For each sample and feature in the data set, an associated SHAP value quantifies the contribution of that feature to the prediction of that sample. Analysis of these SHAP values provides valuable insight into the model's decision-making process, which can be leveraged to create data-driven solutions. The interpretation of these SHAP values, however, is model-dependent, so there does not exist a universal analysis procedure. To aid in these efforts, we present a detailed investigation of SHAP analysis across various machine learning models and data sets. In uncovering the details and nuance behind SHAP analysis, we hope to empower analysts in this less-explored territory. We also present a novel generalization of the waterfall plot to the multi-classification problem.

</details>


### [153] [Tracking Adaptation Time: Metrics for Temporal Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07266)
*Lorenzo Iovine,Giacomo Ziffer,Emanuele Della Valle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Evaluating robustness under temporal distribution shift remains an open challenge. Existing metrics quantify the average decline in performance, but fail to capture how models adapt to evolving data. As a result, temporal degradation is often misinterpreted: when accuracy declines, it is unclear whether the model is failing to adapt or whether the data itself has become inherently more challenging to learn. In this work, we propose three complementary metrics to distinguish adaptation from intrinsic difficulty in the data. Together, these metrics provide a dynamic and interpretable view of model behavior under temporal distribution shift. Results show that our metrics uncover adaptation patterns hidden by existing analysis, offering a richer understanding of temporal robustness in evolving environments.

</details>


### [154] [SL-FAC: A Communication-Efficient Split Learning Framework with Frequency-Aware Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07316)
*Zehang Lin,Miao Yang,Haihan Zhu,Zheng Lin,Jianhao Huang,Jing Yang,Guangjin Pan,Dianxin Luan,Zihan Fang,Shunzhi Zhu,Wei Ni,John Thompson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The growing complexity of neural networks hinders the deployment of distributed machine learning on resource-constrained devices. Split learning (SL) offers a promising solution by partitioning the large model and offloading the primary training workload from edge devices to an edge server. However, the increasing number of participating devices and model complexity leads to significant communication overhead from the transmission of smashed data (e.g., activations and gradients), which constitutes a critical bottleneck for SL. To tackle this challenge, we propose SL-FAC, a communication-efficient SL framework comprising two key components: adaptive frequency decomposition (AFD) and frequency-based quantization compression (FQC). AFD first transforms the smashed data into the frequency domain and decomposes it into spectral components with distinct information. FQC then applies customized quantization bit widths to each component based on its spectral energy distribution. This collaborative approach enables SL-FAC to achieve significant communication reduction while strategically preserving the information most crucial for model convergence. Extensive experiments confirm the superior performance of SL-FAC for improving the training efficiency.

</details>


### [155] [How to sketch a learning algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07328)
*Sam Gunn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究深度学习中的数据删除问题，旨在快速预测删除部分训练数据后模型的行为。为实现这一目标，研究者提出了一种基于"稳定性"假设的数据删除方案，能够以极小误差ε预测模型输出，且计算效率仅比常规训练和推理慢poly(1/ε)倍，存储需求为poly(1/ε)个模型。该方法核心在于通过在随机复方向上计算高阶导数来局部刻画算术电路，结合前向模式自动微分实现高效计算。实验验证了稳定性假设与强大AI模型的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据的选择如何影响AI模型是解释性、隐私保护和基础科学的核心问题。数据删除问题的本质是：在完成一定预计算后，能否快速预测当某一子集训练数据被排除时，模型在给定情境下的行为表现。

Method: 本文提出一种数据删除方案，核心技术包括：(1) 引入"稳定性"(stability)假设，比先前工作的假设更具一般性；(2) 通过在随机复方向上计算高阶导数实现算术电路的局部刻画；(3) 利用前向模式自动微分廉价计算这些高阶导数；(4) 预计算poly(1/ε)个模型用于后续预测。

Result: 方案可实现误差ε任意小（vanishing error ε）的预测精度。效率方面：预计算和预测算法仅比常规训练和推理慢poly(1/ε)因子；存储需求为poly(1/ε)个模型。在microgpt上的最小实验集验证了稳定性假设的可行性。

Conclusion: 稳定性假设与强大的AI模型完全兼容，这是相对于先前工作的关键优势。代码已开源，为数据删除问题的实际应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: How does the choice of training data influence an AI model? This question is of central importance to interpretability, privacy, and basic science. At its core is the data deletion problem: after a reasonable amount of precomputation, quickly predict how the model would behave in a given situation if a given subset of training data had been excluded from the learning algorithm.
  We present a data deletion scheme capable of predicting model outputs with vanishing error $\varepsilon$ in the deep learning setting. Our precomputation and prediction algorithms are only $\mathrm{poly}(1/\varepsilon)$ factors slower than regular training and inference, respectively. The storage requirements are those of $\mathrm{poly}(1/\varepsilon)$ models.
  Our proof is based on an assumption that we call "stability." In contrast to the assumptions made by prior work, stability appears to be fully compatible with learning powerful AI models. In support of this, we show that stability is satisfied in a minimal set of experiments with microgpt. Our code is available at https://github.com/SamSpo1/microgpt-sketch.
  At a technical level, our work is based on a new method for locally sketching an arithmetic circuit by computing higher-order derivatives in random complex directions. Forward-mode automatic differentiation allows cheap computation of these derivatives.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

### [156] [ForkKV: Scaling Multi-LoRA Agent Serving via Copy-on-Write Disaggregated KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06370)
*Shao Wang,Rui Ren,Lin Gui*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ForkKV is a serving system for multi-LLoRA agent workflows that uses OS fork with copy-on-write semantics to address KV cache memory bottlenecks. It achieves up to 3.0x throughput improvement through a DualRadixTree architecture and specialized ResidualAttention kernel.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The shift to complex multi-agent LLM workflows with LoRA adaptation creates a critical memory bottleneck: unique LoRA activations cause KV cache divergence, making traditional prefix caching ineffective and leading to redundant KV cache maintenance that rapidly saturates GPU capacity.

Method: ForkKV applies OS fork with copy-on-write semantics to KV cache management: (1) DualRadixTree architecture physically decouples KV cache into massive shared component and lightweight agent-specific components; (2) ResidualAttention kernel reconstructs disaggregated KV cache directly within on-chip SRAM for efficient execution.

Result: Evaluations across diverse language models and practical datasets demonstrate up to 3.0x throughput improvement over state-of-the-art multi-LoRA serving systems with negligible impact on generation quality.

Conclusion: ForkKV successfully addresses the KV cache memory footprint bottleneck in multi-LLoRA serving through its CoW-inspired memory management paradigm, enabling efficient shared context utilization across specialized agents.

Abstract: The serving paradigm of large language models (LLMs) is rapidly shifting towards complex multi-agent workflows where specialized agents collaborate over massive shared contexts. While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables the efficient co-hosting of these specialized agents on a single base model, it introduces a critical memory footprint bottleneck during serving. Specifically, unique LoRA activations cause Key-Value (KV) cache divergence across agents, rendering traditional prefix caching ineffective for shared contexts. This forces redundant KV cache maintenance, rapidly saturating GPU capacity and degrading throughput.
  To address this challenge, we introduce ForkKV, a serving system for multi-LoRA agent workflows centered around a novel memory management paradigm in OS: fork with copy-on-write (CoW). By exploiting the structural properties of LoRA, ForkKV physically decouples the KV cache into a massive shared component (analogous to the parent process's memory pages) and lightweight agent-specific components (the child process's pages). To support this mechanism, we propose a DualRadixTree architecture that allows newly forked agents to inherit the massive shared cache and apply CoW semantics for their lightweight unique cache. Furthermore, to guarantee efficient execution, we design ResidualAttention, a specialized kernel that reconstructs the disaggregated KV cache directly within on-chip SRAM. Comprehensive evaluations across diverse language models and practical datasets of different tasks demonstrate that ForkKV achieves up to 3.0x the throughput of state-of-the-art multi-LoRA serving systems with a negligible impact on generation quality.

</details>


### [157] [DynLP: Parallel Dynamic Batch Update for Label Propagation in Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06596)
*S M Shovan,Arindam Khanda,S M Ferdous,Sajal K. Das,Mahantesh Halappanavar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出DynLP，一种针对图上半监督学习的动态批处理并行标签传播算法，用于处理增量式到达的数据批次，避免传统方法的全图重计算问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中数据通常以增量批次形式到达，传统的标签传播算法在新批次到来时需要重新计算所有标签，造成冗余计算和效率低下。现有方法缺乏高效的增量更新机制。

Method: 提出DynLP算法，一种以GPU为中心的动态批处理并行标签传播方法。该方法仅对必要部分进行更新，将变化传播到相关子图而无需全图重计算，并利用GPU架构优化实现高效并行处理。

Result: 在大规模数据集上，相比现有最优方法平均实现13倍加速，最多可达102倍加速。

Conclusion: DynLP通过增量更新策略和GPU并行化有效解决了批量数据场景下的标签传播效率问题，为大规模图数据的动态学习提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning aims to infer class labels using only a small fraction of labeled data. In graph-based semi-supervised learning, this is typically achieved through label propagation to predict labels of unlabeled nodes. However, in real-world applications, data often arrive incrementally in batches. Each time a new batch appears, reapplying the traditional label propagation algorithm to recompute all labels is redundant, computationally intensive, and inefficient. To address the absence of an efficient label propagation update method, we propose DynLP, a novel GPU-centric Dynamic Batched Parallel Label Propagation algorithm that performs only the necessary updates, propagating changes to the relevant subgraph without requiring full recalculation. By exploiting GPU architectural optimizations, our algorithm achieves on average 13x and upto 102x speedup on large-scale datasets compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [158] [Sparsity-Aware Roofline Models for Sparse Matrix-Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06637)
*Matthew Qian,Yahia Ramadan,Suhita Anubha,Ariful Azad*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了屋顶线模型(Roofline Model)在稀疏矩阵-稠密矩阵乘法(SpMM)性能预测中的适用性与局限性，发现传统单一屋顶线模型无法准确预测不同稀疏结构下的性能，需要针对矩阵稀疏结构特点设计专用的屋顶线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SpMM是科学计算、图分析和机器学习中的关键内核，其性能通常受内存带宽限制。传统屋顶线模型未考虑矩阵稀疏结构对算术强度和可达到性能的影响，因此需要研究稀疏结构感知的屋顶线建模方法。

Method: 1. 评估三种SpMM实现：CSR(压缩稀疏行)、CSB(压缩稀疏块)和Intel MKL；2. 使用SuiteSparse矩阵库中的大规模矩阵，按稀疏模式分组：块结构、带状(对角)、无标度和均匀随机矩阵；3. 推导包含内存流量、缓存局部性和分块行为的稀疏感知屋顶线模型；4. 在AMD Perlmutter计算节点上测试不同稠密矩阵列数的性能。

Result: 分块和结构化稀疏性显著改变有效算术强度；单一屋顶线模型无法准确预测不同稀疏结构下的性能；数据布局和分块策略需要结合矩阵结构进行评估，而非使用统一模型。

Conclusion: 准确的SpMM屋顶线性能分析需要稀疏感知建模；数据布局和分块策略的有效性取决于矩阵结构特征，应针对不同稀疏模式采用差异化的性能优化方法。

Abstract: Sparse matrix-dense matrix multiplication (SpMM) is a critical kernel in scientific computing, graph analytics, and machine learning, whose performance is often constrained by memory bandwidth. In this work, we investigate the applicability and limitations of roofline modeling for SpMM by explicitly accounting for the impact of matrix sparsity structure on arithmetic intensity and attainable performance. We evaluate three SpMM implementations: Compressed Sparse Row (CSR), Compressed Sparse Blocks (CSB), and Intel's Math Kernel Library (MKL). Each implementation was tested using large-scale matrices from the SuiteSparse collection and grouped by sparsity pattern, including block-structured, banded (diagonal), scale-free, and uniformly random matrices. We derive sparsity-aware roofline models that incorporate memory traffic, cache locality, and blocking behavior, and demonstrate that a single model is insufficient to accurately predict performance across diverse structures. Experiments were conducted on an AMD-based Perlmutter compute node with a varying number of columns in the dense matrix. In particular, blocking and structured sparsity significantly alter effective arithmetic intensity. The results show that accurate roofline-based performance analysis of SpMM requires sparsity-aware modeling, and that data layout and blocking strategies must be evaluated in the context of matrix structure rather than through a single unified model.

</details>


### [159] [Foundry: Template-Based CUDA Graph Context Materialization for Fast LLM Serving Cold Start](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06664)
*Xueshen Liu,Yongji Wu,Yuncheng Yao,Danyang Zhuo,Ion Stoica,Z. Morley Mao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Foundry是一个基于模板的CUDA图上下文物化系统，通过离线保存图拓扑和执行上下文，在线重建可执行图，从而将LLM冷启动延迟降低最多99%，将Qwen3-235B-A22B的初始化时间从10分钟缩短至3.9秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM服务依赖autoscaling和并行重配置应对动态负载，但冷启动延迟仍是主要瓶颈。虽然模型权重加载已降至秒级，但CUDA图捕获仍需数十秒到数分钟，常主导启动时间。现有方案存在缺陷：内核特定修补脆弱，进程级checkpoint/restore重量级且缺乏灵活性。

Method: Foundry在离线阶段持久化图拓扑和执行上下文（含设备地址和延迟加载的内核代码），在线以极低开销重建可执行图。系统强制确定性内存布局，自动提取和重载图所需的内核二进制，通过拓扑模板化降低重建成本。针对分布式服务，单GPU离线捕获即可生成多GPU部署模板，仅需修补rank相关的通信状态。

Result: 在高达235B参数的稠密和MoE模型上，Foundry将冷启动延迟降低最多99%，Qwen3-235B-A22B初始化时间从10分钟降至3.9秒，同时保持CUDA图的吞吐量收益。

Conclusion: Foundry成功解决了CUDA图冷启动开销大的核心问题，通过模板化上下文物化实现了近乎零成本的图重建，为大规模LLM服务的高效弹性扩缩容提供了关键技术支撑。

Abstract: Modern LLM service providers increasingly rely on autoscaling and parallelism reconfiguration to respond to rapidly changing workloads, but cold-start latency remains a major bottleneck. While recent systems have reduced model weight loading to seconds, CUDA graph capture still takes tens of seconds to minutes and often dominates startup. Unfortunately, CUDA graphs cannot be naively serialized: beyond graph topology, they are tightly coupled to execution context, including device addresses embedded in kernel arguments and kernel code lazily loaded during warmup. Existing approaches either rely on brittle kernel-specific patching or heavyweight process-level checkpoint/restore that are inflexible to dynamic parallelism switching. We present Foundry, a template-based CUDA graph context materialization system that persists both graph topology and execution context during an offline processing stage, and reconstructs executable graphs online with negligible overhead. Foundry enforces deterministic memory layouts, automatically extracts and reloads kernel binaries required by captured graphs, and reduces online reconstruction costs through topology-based templating. For distributed serving, Foundry further enables a single-GPU offline capture to generate templates for multi-GPU deployments by patching only rank-dependent communication state. Across dense and MoE models up to 235B parameters, Foundry reduces cold-start latency by up to 99%, cutting the initialization time of Qwen3-235B-A22B from 10 minutes to 3.9 seconds while preserving the throughput gains of CUDA graphs.

</details>


### [160] [Beyond End-to-End: Dynamic Chain Optimization for Private LLM Adaptation on the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06819)
*Yebo Wu,Jingguang Li,Chunlin Tian,Kahou Tam,Zhijiang Guo,Li Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ChainFed提出一种链式联邦微调范式，通过逐层顺序训练和冻结适配器来突破边缘设备的内存限制，包含三项核心技术：动态层协同微调、全局感知优化和函数导向自适应微调，实验表明该方法最高可提升平均准确率46.46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调虽能保护隐私地适配LLM，但边缘设备内存有限，无法承担LLM的高内存需求，传统端到端更新方法在资源受限场景下难以实施。

Method: ChainFed采用链式训练策略：首先训练初始适配器至收敛，冻结其权重，再进行下一层的训练。此外集成三项技术：1）动态层协同微调（Dynamic Layer Co-Tuning）弥合顺序微调层间的语义鸿沟；2）全局感知优化（Globally Perceptive Optimization）使各适配器具备超越局部目标的全局视野；3）函数导向自适应微调（Function-Oriented Adaptive Tuning）自动识别最优微调起点。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明ChainFed优于现有方法，最高可提升平均准确率46.46%。

Conclusion: ChainFed通过链式逐层训练策略有效解决了联邦学习中边缘设备的内存瓶颈问题，三项核心技术的集成使其在任务适应能力上显著超越现有联邦微调方法。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning enables privacy-preserving LLM adaptation but faces a critical bottleneck: the disparity between LLMs' high memory demands and edge devices' limited capacity. To break the memory barrier, we propose Chain Federated Fine-Tuning (ChainFed), an innovative paradigm that forgoes end-to-end updates in favor of a sequential, layer-by-layer manner. It first trains the initial adapter to convergence, freezes its weights, and then proceeds to the next. This iterative train-and-freeze process forms an optimization chain, gradually enhancing the model's task-specific proficiency. ChainFed further integrates three core techniques: 1) Dynamic Layer Co-Tuning to bridge semantic gaps between sequentially tuned layers and facilitate information flow; 2) Globally Perceptive Optimization to endow each adapter with foresight beyond its local objective; 3) Function-Oriented Adaptive Tuning to automatically identify the optimal fine-tuning starting point. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of ChainFed over existing methods, boosting average accuracy by up to 46.46\%.

</details>


### [161] [Exploiting Aggregate Programming in a Multi-Robot Service Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06876)
*Giorgio Audrito,Andrea Basso,Daniele Bortoluzzi,Ferruccio Damiani,Giordano Scarso,Gianluca Torta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于聚合编程（AP）的多机器人服务系统原型，旨在解决多机器人系统协调中的复杂性挑战，并通过模拟实验和大学图书馆实地测试进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在医疗、探索、救援等领域应用广泛，但构建这类系统面临物理复杂性和分布式协调的双重挑战，需要新的设计方法来实现弹性协调。

Method: 采用聚合编程（AP）方法设计多机器人协调软件，构建系统原型，并分别通过仿真环境和大学图书馆真实场景进行测试验证。

Result: 成功实现了多机器人服务系统原型，验证了AP方法在多机器人协调中的可行性，实现在 proximity-based 通信环境下的弹性分布式协调。

Conclusion: 聚合编程为多机器人系统的协调软件设计提供了有效解决方案，能够应对物理机器人及其环境的复杂性挑战，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems are becoming increasingly relevant within diverse application domains, such as healthcare, exploration, and rescue missions. However, building such systems is still a significant challenge, since it adds the complexities of the physical nature of robots and their environments to those inherent in coordinating any distributed (multi-agent) system. Aggregate Programming (AP) has recently emerged as a promising approach to engineering resilient, distributed systems with proximity-based communication, and is notably supported by practical frameworks.  In this paper we present a prototype of a multi-robot service system, which adopts AP for the design and implementation of its coordination software. The prototype has been validated both with simulations, and with tests in a University library.

</details>


### [162] [On the Decidability of Distributed Tasks with Output Sets under Asynchrony and Any Number of Crashes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06920)
*Timothé Albouy,Antonio Fernández Anta,Chryssis Georgiou,Nicolas Nicolaou,Junlang Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文定义了SOS任务（输出集合集合任务）这一新的分布式任务类别，并证明该类别在异步系统且最多t个进程崩溃的条件下是可判定的：t=0时所有SOS任务可解；t>0时，SOS任务可解当且仅当其SOS图（以输出集合为顶点、包含关系为边的图）是连通的。一个惊人推论是：对于k>1的k-集合共识在任何崩溃数下均可解，仅k=1（共识）在t>0时不可解。此外，还研究了要求始终产生d个不同输出值的d-不一致任务族，其可实现条件与调和级数相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式计算中一个核心问题是判定哪些并发任务在给定故障模型下是否可解。该研究旨在建立一类新的分布式任务（SOS任务）的可解性判定理论，提供有效的判定程序。

Method: 通过构建SOS图（顶点为输出集合，边表示包含关系），将任务可解性判定转化为图论问题；利用容错异步模型进行分析。

Result: （1）SOS任务类是可判定的，存在有效判定程序；（2）t=0时所有SOS任务可解；（3）t>0时SOS任务可解当且仅当SOS图G=(O,⊂)连通；（4）k-集合共识：k>1时在任意崩溃数下可解，k=1（共识）仅在t=0时可解；（5）d-不一致任务的实现条件与调和级数相关。

Conclusion: SOS任务类的可解性完全由其SOS图的连通性决定，共识是唯一在存在崩溃时不可解的k-集合共识特例（k=1），而k>1的集合共识在任何故障数量下均可解。

Abstract: In this paper, we define a new class of distributed tasks, called SOS tasks (for Set of Output Sets tasks), defined by the set $O$ of distinct output sets of values that can be produced. We then demonstrate that this class of tasks is decidable: there exists an effective procedure that determines whether any SOS task is solvable asynchronously under $t$ crashes. The decision rule is as follows. Every SOS task is solvable when $t=0$. For $t > 0$, an SOS task is solvable if and only if its SOS graph $G=(O,\subset)$ is connected. In this graph, each vertex is an output set in $O$, and two vertices are linked by an edge whenever one output set includes the other. One of the surprising implications of our results is that, without a validity property, $k$-set agreement is solvable under any number of crashes $t \geq 0$ for $k>1$, and unsolvable under $t >0$ crashes only for $k=1$ (consensus). Finally, we study a novel family of tasks called $d$-disagreement, which requires the system to always produce $d$ different output values, and we show that its implementability condition is related to the harmonic series.

</details>


### [163] [NestPipe: Large-Scale Recommendation Training on 1,500+ Accelerators via Nested Pipelining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06956)
*Zhida Jiang,Zhaolong Xing,Huichao Chai,Tianxing Sun,Qiang Peng,Baopeng Yuan,Jiaxing Wang,Hua Du,Zhixin Wu,Xuemiao Li,Yikui Cao,Xinyu Liu,Yongxiang Feng,Zhen Chen,Ke Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: NestPipe通过层级化稀疏并行策略解决大规模分布式推荐系统训练中的数据移动瓶颈，在保证同步训练语义的前提下，在1,536个worker上实现最高3.06倍加速和94.07%的扩展效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐模型参数量已达万亿级别，集群规模扩展至O(1k)时，训练瓶颈从计算和内存转移到数据移动，特别是嵌入表的查询和通信延迟。现有方案仅优化单一瓶颈或以牺牲训练一致性为代价提升吞吐。

Method: 提出基于嵌套流水线的层级化稀疏并行框架NestPipe：(1) 批次间层：双缓冲流水线(DBP)通过双缓冲同步构建无过期五阶段流水线，消除嵌入表过期问题；(2) 批次内层：识别嵌入冻结现象，提出冻结窗口流水线(FWP)，通过协调流调度和基于键的样本聚类重叠All2All通信与密集计算。

Result: 在1,536个worker的生产级GPU和NPU集群上实验验证，NestPipe实现最高3.06倍加速和94.07%的扩展效率。

Conclusion: NestPipe成功在保持同步训练语义的同时，同时解决了嵌入表查询瓶颈和通信瓶颈，为大规模分布式嵌入训练提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Modern recommendation models have increased to trillions of parameters. As cluster scales expand to O(1k), distributed training bottlenecks shift from computation and memory to data movement, especially lookup and communication latency associated with embeddings. Existing solutions either optimize only one bottleneck or improve throughput by sacrificing training consistency. This paper presents NestPipe, a large-scale decentralized embedding training framework that tackles both bottlenecks while preserving synchronous training semantics. NestPipe exploits two hierarchical sparse parallelism opportunities through nested pipelining. At the inter-batch level, Dual-Buffer Pipelining (DBP) constructs a staleness-free five-stage pipeline through dual-buffer synchronization, mitigating lookup bottlenecks without embedding staleness. At the intra-batch level, we identify the embedding freezing phenomenon, which inspires Frozen-Window Pipelining (FWP) to overlap All2All communication with dense computation via coordinated stream scheduling and key-centric sample clustering. Experiments on production GPU and NPU clusters with 1,536 workers demonstrate that NestPipe achieves up to 3.06x speedup and 94.07% scaling efficiency.

</details>


### [164] [Scheduling the Unschedulable: Taming Black-Box LLM Inference at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06970)
*Renzhong Yuan,Yijun Zeng,Xiaosong Gao,Linxi Yu,Haochun Liao,Han Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了客户端如何利用输出token计数的先验知识来调度对黑盒LLM API的请求，提出allocation-ordering-overload control三层分解框架。实验表明，粗粒度幅度先验是实现有效客户端控制的关键阈值，在高拥塞下可实现100%完成率和100%截止期限满足率，且在高达60%乘法预测误差下仍能优雅降级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当客户端可在提交时预测输出token数量时，对黑盒LLM API的调度变为"半先知"问题——决策可基于粗粒度token先验，但提供商内部仍不可见。如何在黑盒约束下实现有效调度控制成为关键挑战。

Method: 提出三层分解框架：(1) Allocation层 - 通过自适应DRR实现类间份额分配；(2) Ordering层 - 基于可行集评分的类内排序；(3) Overload Control层 - 基于成本阶梯的显式 admit/defer/reject 控制。信息阶梯实验评估幅度先验的价值。

Result: 粗粒度幅度先验（非仅类标签）是实现有效客户端控制的关键阈值；移除幅度信息使短请求P95延迟增加高达5.8倍。平衡/高拥塞下实现100%完成率、100%截止期限满足率，有效goodput达4.2±1.6 SLO达标请求/秒；在高达60%乘法预测误差下仍可优雅降级。Fair Queuing相比FIFO使短请求P90改善+32%（仅增加长请求开销+17%），优于Short-Priority的+27%/+116%权衡。

Conclusion: 三层客户端分解框架有效支持不同公平性目标（无需更改剩余栈）；成本阶梯丢弃策略与服务质量目标可解释地关联；在重请求主导场景下策略在完成率、尾延迟和可解释丢弃上表现差异明显。

Abstract: When output token counts can be predicted at submission time (Gan et al., 2026), client-side scheduling against a black-box LLM API becomes semi-clairvoyant: decisions condition on coarse token priors even though the provider's internals remain hidden. We decompose this boundary problem into three separable concerns: allocation (inter-class share via adaptive DRR), ordering (intra-class sequencing with feasible-set scoring), and overload control (explicit admit/defer/reject on a cost ladder). An information ladder experiment shows that coarse magnitude priors -- not class labels alone -- are the practical threshold for useful client control; removing magnitude inflates short-request P95 by up to $5.8\times$ and degrades deadline satisfaction. Under balanced / high congestion the full stack achieves 100% completion, 100% deadline satisfaction, and useful goodput of $4.2 \pm 1.6$ SLO-meeting requests/s with short P95 within tens of milliseconds of quota-tiered isolation. A predictor-noise sweep confirms graceful degradation under up to 60% multiplicative error. Heavy-dominated regimes separate policies on completion, tail, and interpretable shedding. We further compare short-priority allocation (biased toward interactive traffic) with Fair Queuing (round-robin across classes): Fair Queuing achieves +32% short-request P90 improvement over FIFO with only +17% long-request overhead, versus Short-Priority's +27% / +116% trade-off -- demonstrating that the allocation layer accommodates different fairness objectives without changing the remaining stack. We contribute the three-layer client-side decomposition, controlled evaluation of joint metrics across regimes, allocation-policy alternatives, and overload-policy evidence linking cost-ladder shedding to the stated service objective.

</details>


### [165] [Autopoiesis: A Self-Evolving System Paradigm for LLM Serving Under Runtime Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07144)
*Youhe Jiang,Ran Yan,You Peng,Wenshuang Li,Taiyi Wang,Fangcheng Fu,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Autopoiesis是一个新型的在线自进化LLM服务系统，将LLM服务从静态策略部署转变为持续在线策略演化。该系统通过LLM驱动的程序合成工作流，根据实时观测的运行时动态演化服务策略，使策略能够持续观察真实系统行为并在运行时条件变化时重写策略代码，实现对动态运行时条件的自主适应。实验表明该系统相比现有最优LLM服务系统可提升性能最高达53%，平均提升34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM服务系统依赖静态、人工设计的策略（如调度算法和重调度策略）来应对运行时动态（如工作负载波动和弹性集群自动扩缩容）。然而，这些策略面临复杂的权衡取舍（如调度开销vs执行效率、重调度频率vs重配置开销），最优平衡点随工作负载和运行时条件持续变化，固定策略无法自适应。

Method: 1. LLM驱动的程序合成工作流：根据实时观测的动态演化服务策略，策略能够反映在复杂多维权衡空间中的最优决策；2. 持续运行时合成：系统在服务过程中持续运行，观察真实系统行为并重写策略代码，使策略设计从一次性离线工作转变为持续运行的系统组件。

Result: 实验评估涵盖多种运行时动态场景，相比现有最优LLM服务系统实现最高53%、平均34%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 提出了LLM服务的新范式：服务策略不再是部署前由人类设计的静态产物，而是LLM在部署过程中持续演化的"活代码"，用于导航超出人类设计能力的运行时权衡。

Abstract: Modern Large Language Model (LLM) serving operates in highly volatile environments characterized by severe runtime dynamics, such as workload fluctuations and elastic cluster autoscaling. Traditional serving systems rely on static, human-engineered serving policies (e.g., scheduling algorithms and rescheduling strategies) to manage these dynamics. However, these policies must navigate deeply intertwined runtime trade-offs (e.g., scheduling overhead vs. execution efficiency, rescheduling frequency vs. reconfiguration overhead), whose optimal balance is workload-specific and shifts continuously as runtime conditions evolve, rendering any fixed policy fundamentally unable to adapt. We propose Autopoiesis, a novel online self-evolving system that shifts LLM serving from static policy deployment to continuous online policy evolution. First, Autopoiesis introduces an LLM-driven program synthesis workflow to evolve serving policies with respect to real-time observed dynamics, where the evolved policies reflect the optimal decision in navigating the complex, multi-dimensional trade-off space. Second, Autopoiesis enables this synthesis process to operate continuously during serving, observing real-world system behavior, and rewriting the policy code as runtime trade-offs shift, thereby transforming policy design from a one-time offline endeavor into an ongoing system component, enabling autonomous adaptation to evolving runtime conditions. Together, we establish a new paradigm: Serving policies are no longer static artifacts designed by humans before deployment, but living code that LLMs continuously evolve throughout deployment to navigate runtime trade-offs beyond human design. We evaluate Autopoiesis across diverse runtime dynamics and show up to 53% and on average 34% improvements over state-of-the-art LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [166] [InfiniLoRA: Disaggregated Multi-LoRA Serving for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07173)
*Hongyu Chen,Letian Ruan,Zilin Xu,Yuchen Li,Xinyu Chen,Jingwen Leng,Bingsheng He,Minyi Guo,Shixuan Sun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: InfiniLoRA是一个解耦的LoRA服务系统，用于解决MoE架构下LoRA内存成本高、尾延迟大的问题。通过共享LoRA服务器、并行感知执行、SLO驱动配置和关键路径优化（GPU启动通信、硬件专用内核），在严格延迟SLO下实现3.05倍请求率提升，54% SLO达标率改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着MoE等新型模型架构的出现，LoRA的内存成本显著增加，现有耦合式LoRA服务设计扩展性差且容易出现尾延迟膨胀问题。

Method: InfiniLoRA将LoRA执行与基础模型推理解耦，引入了具备并行感知执行的共享LoRA服务器、SLO驱动的资源配置，以及关键路径优化（包括GPU启动的通信机制和硬件专用LoRA内核）。

Result: 在严格延迟SLO条件下，InfiniLoRA可实现平均3.05倍的服务请求率提升，LoRA适配器满足SLO要求的比例提高54.0%。

Conclusion: 解耦式LoRA服务设计能有效解决MoE架构下的扩展性问题，InfiniLoRA通过多项优化技术显著提升了系统在多租户多任务场景下的服务能力。

Abstract: LoRA enables efficient customization of LLMs and is widely used in multi-tenant and multi-task serving. However, emerging model architectures such as MoE significantly increase LoRA memory cost, making existing coupled LoRA serving designs poorly scalable and prone to tail-latency inflation. We present InfiniLoRA, a disaggregated LoRA serving system that decouples LoRA execution from base-model inference. InfiniLoRA introduces a shared LoRA Server with parallelism-aware execution, SLO-driven provisioning, and critical-path optimizations, including GPU-initiated communication and hardware-specialized LoRA kernels. Experiments show that InfiniLoRA can achieve an average $3.05\times$ increase in serviceable request rate under strict latency SLOs, and improve the percentage of LoRA adapters satisfying the SLO requirement by 54.0\%.

</details>

### [167] [EviSnap: Faithful Evidence-Cited Explanations for Cold-Start Cross-Domain Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06172)
*Yingjun Dai,Ahmed El-Roby*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Cold-start cross-domain recommender (CDR) systems predict a user's preferences in a target domain using only their source-domain behavior, yet existing CDR models either map opaque embeddings or rely on post-hoc or LLM-generated rationales that are hard to audit. We introduce EviSnap a lightweight CDR framework whose predictions are explained by construction with evidence-cited, faithful rationales. EviSnap distills noisy reviews into compact facet cards using an LLM offline, pairing each facet with verbatim supporting sentences. It then induces a shared, domain-agnostic concept bank by clustering facet embeddings and computes user-positive, user-negative, and item-presence concept activations via evidence-weighted pooling. A single linear concept-to-concept map transfers users across domains, and a linear scoring head yields per-concept additive contributions, enabling exact score decompositions and counterfactual 'what-if' edits grounded in the cited sentences. Experiments on the Amazon Reviews dataset across six transfers among Books, Movies, and Music show that EviSnap consistently outperforms strong mapping and review-text baselines while passing deletion- and sufficiency-based tests for explanation faithfulness.

</details>


### [168] [Beyond Case Law: Evaluating Structure-Aware Retrieval and Safety in Statute-Centric Legal QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06173)
*Kyubyung Chae,Jewon Yeom,Jeongjae Park,Seunghyun Bae,Ijun Jang,Hyunbin Jin,Jinkwan Jang,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
