Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Legal QA benchmarks have predominantly focused on case law, overlooking the unique challenges of statute-centric regulatory reasoning. In statutory domains, relevant evidence is distributed across hierarchically linked documents, creating a statutory retrieval gap where conventional retrievers fail and models often hallucinate under incomplete context. We introduce SearchFireSafety, a structure- and safety-aware benchmark for statute-centric legal QA. Instantiated on fire-safety regulations as a representative case, the benchmark evaluates whether models can retrieve hierarchically fragmented evidence and safely abstain when statutory context is insufficient. SearchFireSafety adopts a dual-source evaluation framework combining real-world questions that require citation-aware retrieval and synthetic partial-context scenarios that stress-test hallucination and refusal behavior. Experiments across multiple large language models show that graph-guided retrieval substantially improves performance, but also reveal a critical safety trade-off: domain-adapted models are more likely to hallucinate when key statutory evidence is missing. Our findings highlight the need for benchmarks that jointly evaluate hierarchical retrieval and model safety in statute-centric regulatory settings.

</details>


### [169] [Robustness Risk of Conversational Retrieval: Identifying and Mitigating Noise Sensitivity in Qwen3-Embedding Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06176)
*Weishu Chen,Zhouhui Hou,Mingjie Zhan,Zhicheng Zhao,Fei Su*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了Qwen3-embedding模型在对话式检索场景中的一个关键鲁棒性漏洞：当查询无提示直接检索时，结构化对话噪声会不成比例地被检索出来，尽管其语义信息价值极低。该问题在不同模型规模上均稳定出现，在标准干净查询基准测试中难以察觉，但在对话检索场景下显著恶化。研究进一步发现，轻量级查询提示可有效抑制噪声入侵并恢复排序稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对话式检索系统的评估与真实部署场景存在显著差距。实际对话查询往往短小、弱指定且缺乏显式意图表达，而现有基准测试主要采用干净、规范的查询表述。此外，真实检索语料库中普遍存在结构化的对话伪影（如对话标记、元信息等），这些非语义内容可能在检索过程中产生误导性匹配。该研究旨在填补这一评估空白，系统评估Qwen3-embedding模型在真实对话环境下的鲁棒性表现。

Method: 研究聚焦于Qwen3-embedding系列模型，构建了模拟真实对话检索场景的实验环境。具体包括：使用短小、对话式的弱指定查询作为输入；检索语料库中引入结构化的对话式噪声（如对话标记、轮次信息等）；对比分析不同模型规模（Qwen3系列）的表现；与早期Qwen变体及其他主流稠密检索基线模型进行横向对比；测试轻量级查询提示（query prompting）对检索行为的调节作用。

Result: 研究发现揭示了三项关键发现：（1）鲁棒性漏洞：在无查询提示的对话检索条件下，结构化对话风格噪声尽管语义信息贫乏，却能被不成比例地高检索率，入侵排序前列结果；（2）跨规模一致性：该失败模式在不同Qwen3模型规模上均稳定出现；（3）基准盲区：此问题在标准干净查询基准测试中基本不可见，难以被传统评估协议捕获；（4）对比恶化：Qwen3的表现显著差于早期Qwen变体及其他主流稠密检索基线；（5）提示有效性：轻量级查询提示可显著改变检索行为，有效抑制噪声入侵并恢复排序稳定性。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了对话检索系统中一个尚未被充分认识的鲁棒性风险，表明模型在语义丰富内容与结构化噪声之间的判别能力存在缺陷。研究强调了开发反映真实部署复杂性的评估协议的重要性，仅依赖干净查询基准测试可能掩盖潜在的部署风险。此外，轻量级查询提示作为一种简单有效的干预手段，值得在实际部署中考虑应用。

Abstract: We present an empirical study of embedding-based retrieval under realistic conversational settings, where queries are short, dialogue-like, and weakly specified, and retrieval corpora contain structured conversational artifacts. Focusing on Qwen3-embedding models, we identify a deployment-relevant robustness vulnerability: under conversational retrieval without query prompting, structured dialogue-style noise can become disproportionately retrievable and intrude into top-ranked results, despite being semantically uninformative. This failure mode emerges consistently across model scales, remains largely invisible under standard clean-query benchmarks, and is significantly more pronounced in Qwen3 than in earlier Qwen variants and other widely used dense retrieval baselines. We further show that lightweight query prompting qualitatively alters retrieval behavior, effectively suppressing noise intrusion and restoring ranking stability. Our findings highlight an underexplored robustness risk in conversational retrieval and underscore the importance of evaluation protocols that reflect the complexities of deployed systems.

</details>


### [170] [ARIA: Adaptive Retrieval Intelligence Assistant -- A Multimodal RAG Framework for Domain-Specific Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06179)
*Yue Luo,Dibakar Roy Sarkar,Rachel Herring Sangree,Somdatta Goswami*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Developing effective, domain-specific educational support systems is central to advancing AI in education. Although large language models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable capabilities, they face significant limitations in specialized educational applications, including hallucinations, limited knowledge updates, and lack of domain expertise. Fine-tuning requires complete model retraining, creating substantial computational overhead, while general-purpose LLMs often provide inaccurate responses in specialized contexts due to reliance on generalized training data. To address this, we propose ARIA (Adaptive Retrieval Intelligence Assistant), a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework for creating intelligent teaching assistants across university-level courses. ARIA leverages a multimodal content extraction pipeline combining Docling for structured document analysis, Nougat for mathematical formula recognition, and GPT-4 Vision API for diagram interpretation, with the e5-large-v2 embedding model for high semantic performance and low latency. This enables accurate processing of complex educational materials while maintaining pedagogical consistency through engineered prompts and response controls. We evaluate ARIA using lecture material from Statics and Mechanics of Materials, a sophomore-level civil engineering course at Johns Hopkins University, benchmarking against ChatGPT-5. Results demonstrate 97.5% accuracy in domain-specific question filtering and superior pedagogical performance. ARIA correctly answered all 20 relevant course questions while rejecting 58 of 60 non-relevant queries, achieving 90.9% precision, 100% recall, and 4.89/5.0 average response quality. These findings demonstrate that ARIA's course-agnostic architecture represents a scalable framework for domain-specific educational AI deployment.

</details>


### [171] [The Unreasonable Effectiveness of Data for Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06420)
*Youssef Abdou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究（11个数据集、10种算法组合、9种样本规模）探究了推荐系统中训练数据规模与性能的关系。结果表明，在典型场景下，NDCG@10随数据量增加持续提升，未观察到明显的饱和点；约75%的最大样本量配置取得了各组最佳性能，暗示更多训练数据仍有收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统收集、存储和处理大规模交互数据的成本（时间、能源、计算资源）日益增长，但尚不明确在何时增加数据量将不再产生有意义的收益。

Method: 采用可复现Python评估工作流，使用LensKit和RecBole两个工具包，选取11个大型公开数据集（每个至少700万交互）和10种工具-算法组合，通过绝对分层用户抽样，在10万至1亿交互的9个样本规模下训练模型，测量NDCG@10。

Result: 原始NDCG通常随样本量增加而提升，无明显饱和点；min-max归一化后75%的最大样本点达到组内最佳性能；后期斜率分析（四分位距始终非负，中位数接近1.0）支持上升趋势。

Conclusion: 对于传统推荐系统和典型用户-物品交互数据，增加训练数据仍主要有益；较弱的扩展行为仅出现在非典型数据集和使用RecBole BPR算法的异常情况。

Abstract: In recommender systems, collecting, storing, and processing large-scale interaction data is increasingly costly in terms of time, energy, and computation, yet it remains unclear when additional data stops providing meaningful gains. This paper investigates how offline recommendation performance evolves as the size of the training dataset increases and whether a saturation point can be observed. We implemented a reproducible Python evaluation workflow with two established toolkits, LensKit and RecBole, included 11 large public datasets with at least 7 million interactions, and evaluated 10 tool-algorithm combinations. Using absolute stratified user sampling, we trained models on nine sample sizes from 100,000 to 100,000,000 interactions and measured NDCG@10. Overall, raw NDCG usually increased with sample size, with no observable saturation point. To make result groups comparable, we applied min-max normalization within each group, revealing a clear positive trend in which around 75% of the points at the largest completed sample size also achieved the group's best observed performance. A late-stage slope analysis over the final 10-30% of each group further supported this upward trend: the interquartile range remained entirely non-negative with a median near 1.0. In summary, for traditional recommender systems on typical user-item interaction data, incorporating more training data remains primarily beneficial, while weaker scaling behavior is concentrated in atypical dataset cases and in the algorithmic outlier RecBole BPR under our setup.

</details>


### [172] [CASE: Cadence-Aware Set Encoding for Large-Scale Next Basket Repurchase Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06718)
*Yanan Cao,Ashish Ranjan,Sinduja Subramaniam,Evren Korpeoglu,Kaushiki Nag,Kannan Achan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CASE模型，用于大型零售场景中的下次购买推荐，核心创新在于将基于日历时间的行为节奏(cadence)建模与跨商品交互解耦。传统序列模型按访问顺序处理购物篮，无法显式建模购买间隔天数；CASE将每个商品的购买历史表示为固定时间窗口内的日历时间信号，通过多尺度时间卷积捕捉周期性节奏，并使用次二次复杂度的集合注意力建模商品间依赖关系。在三个公开数据集和一个私有工业数据集上均取得显著提升，生产环境（亿级用户）中top-5 Precision提升8.6%，Recall提升9.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下次购买推荐模型将用户历史表示为按访问顺序索引的离散购物篮序列，这种表示方式无法显式建模流逝的日历时间，也无法在购买间隔天数变化时更新商品排序。然而在频繁补货品类中，用户的购买行为遵循稳定的商品级时间节奏（cadence），建模这种周期性对推荐准确性至关重要。

Method: CASE提出日历时间信号表示法，将每个商品的购买历史建模为固定时间窗口内的日历时间信号；采用共享多尺度时间卷积捕捉周期性购买节奏；使用子二次复杂度的集合注意力机制建模跨商品依赖关系，从而实现生产级可扩展的批量推理。

Result: 在三个公开基准数据集和一个专有数据集上，CASE在多个截断位置的Precision、Recall和NDCG指标上均优于强基线。在生产规模评估中（数千万用户、大规模商品目录），CASE在top-5达到8.6%的相对Precision提升和9.9%的Recall提升。

Conclusion: 可扩展的节奏感知建模在基准测试和工业场景中均能带来显著收益。CASE通过将商品级节奏学习与跨商品交互解耦，实现了显式日历时间建模与生产可扩展性的兼顾。

Abstract: Repurchase behavior is a primary signal in large-scale retail recommendation, particularly in categories with frequent replenishment: many items in a user's next basket were previously purchased and their timing follows stable, item-specific cadences. Yet most next basket repurchase recommendation models represent history as a sequence of discrete basket events indexed by visit order, which cannot explicitly model elapsed calendar time or update item rankings as days pass between purchases. We present CASE (Cadence-Aware Set Encoding for next basket repurchase recommendation), which decouples item-level cadence learning from cross-item interaction, enabling explicit calendar-time modeling while remaining production-scalable. CASE represents each item's purchase history as a calendar-time signal over a fixed horizon, applies shared multi-scale temporal convolutions to capture recurring rhythms, and uses induced set attention to model cross-item dependencies with sub-quadratic complexity, allowing efficient batch inference at scale. Across three public benchmarks and a proprietary dataset, CASE consistently improves Precision, Recall, and NDCG at multiple cutoffs compared to strong next basket prediction baselines. In a production-scale evaluation with tens of millions of users and a large item catalog, CASE achieves up to 8.6% relative Precision and 9.9% Recall lift at top-5, demonstrating that scalable cadence-aware modeling yields measurable gains in both benchmark and industrial settings.

</details>


### [173] [Leveraging LLMs and Heterogeneous Knowledge Graphs for Persona-Driven Session-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06928)
*Muskan Gupta,Suraj Thapa,Jyotsana Khatri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于人格驱动的会话推荐系统(SBRS)框架，通过从不完整会话中推断潜在用户人格，并将其整合到数据驱动的推荐流程中，以解决匿名会话场景下个性化不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有会话推荐系统假设会话是匿名的，限制了个性化能力，尤其在数据稀疏或冷启动场景下表现不佳。LLM虽能生成丰富的物品表示，但因匿名会话限制，难以建模用户人格。用户人格的显式建模对于提升推荐准确性至关重要。

Method: 框架采用两阶段架构：1) 个性化信息提取阶段：构建融合DBpedia元数据的异构图谱(HetKG)，利用LLM派生的物品嵌入初始化图谱，通过异构图谱自监督学习(HDGI)无监督地学习潜在用户人格表示；2) 个性化信息利用阶段：将人格表示与LLM物品嵌入整合到修改后的序列推荐模型中生成候选集，再通过基础序列模型重排序以强调短期会话意图。

Result: 在Amazon Books和Amazon Movies & TV两个数据集上的实验表明，相比基于会话历史的用户嵌入的序列模型，该方法持续提升推荐性能。

Conclusion: 通过将用户人格建模扎根于KG衍生的结构化关系信号，而非仅依赖序列建模或文本用户表示，有效提升了匿名会话场景下的个性化推荐效果。该方法为LLM增强的会话推荐提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Session-based recommendation systems (SBRS) aim to capture user's short-term intent from interaction sequences. However, the common assumption of anonymous sessions limits personalization, particularly under sparse or cold-start conditions. Recent advances in LLM-augmented recommendation have shown that LLMs can generate rich item representations, but modeling user personas with LLMs remains challenging due to anonymous sessions. In this work, we propose a persona-driven SBRS framework that explicitly models latent user personas inferred from a heterogeneous knowledge graph (KG) and integrates them into a data-driven recommendation pipeline.Our framework adopts a two-stage architecture consisting of personalized information extraction and personalized information utilization, inspired by recent chain-of-thought recommendation approaches. In the personalized information extraction stage, we construct a heterogeneous KG that integrates time-independent user-item, item-item, item-feature association, and metadata from DBpedia. We then learn latent user personas in an unsupervised manner using a Heterogeneous Deep Graph Infomax (HDGI) objective over a KG initialized with LLM-derived item embeddings. In the personalized information utilization stage, the learned persona representations together with LLM-derived item embeddings are incorporated into a modified architecture of data-driven SBRS to generate a candidate set of relevant items, followed by reranking using the base sequential model to emphasize short-term session intent. Unlike prior approaches that rely solely on sequence modeling or text-based user representations, our method grounds user persona modeling in structured relational signals derived from a KG. Experiments on Amazon Books and Amazon Movies & TV demonstrate that our approach consistently improves over sequential models with user embeddings derived using session history.

</details>


### [174] [MARVEL: Multimodal Adaptive Reasoning-intensiVe Expand-rerank and retrievaL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07079)
*Mahmoud SalahEldin Kasem,Mohamed Mahmoud,Mostafa Farouk Senussi,Mahmoud Abdalla,Abdelrahman Abdallah,Hyun-Soo Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MARVEL是一个统一的多模态检索框架，通过结合LLM驱动的查询扩展、推理增强的密集检索器(MARVEL-Retriever)和基于GPT-4o的思维链重排序，在MM-BRIGHT基准上达到37.9 nDCG@10，较最佳多模态编码器提升10.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态检索存在三个核心问题：现有方法仅单独解决查询意图扩展、复杂推理检索或重排序中的某一个，而非联合优化；最佳视觉-语言编码器在MM-BRIGHT推理密集型基准上仅达到27.6 nDCG@10，落后于纯文本系统；实际场景需要跨29个技术领域的复杂多模态推理能力。

Method: MARVEL采用三阶段统一流程：(1) LLM驱动查询扩展以捕获潜在意图；(2) MARVEL-Retriever微调密集检索器处理复杂多模态查询；(3) GPT-4o链式思维重排序结合可选多轮 reciprocal rank fusion。在MM-BRIGHT的29个技术领域评估。

Result: MARVEL达到37.9 nDCG@10，较最佳多模态编码器提升+10.3点；在29个领域中的27个领域超越所有单阶段基线方法；在Crypto和Quantum Computing两个高度专业化领域达到匹配或接近最佳基线水平。

Conclusion: 推理密集型多模态检索应通过统一的扩展-检索-重排序框架解决；三个核心能力需要紧密集成而非孤立优化；该工作为复杂多模态推理检索确立了新的性能边界。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval over text corpora remains a fundamental challenge: the best vision-language encoder achieves only 27.6 nDCG@10 on MM-BRIGHT, a reasoning-intensive multimodal retrieval benchmark, underperforming strong text-only systems. We argue that effective multimodal retrieval requires three tightly integrated capabilities that existing approaches address only in isolation: expanding the query's latent intent, retrieving with a model trained for complex reasoning, and reranking via explicit step-by-step reasoning over candidates. We introduce \textbf{MARVEL} (\textbf{M}ultimodal \textbf{A}daptive \textbf{R}easoning-intensi\textbf{V}e \textbf{E}xpand-rerank and retrieva\textbf{L}), a unified pipeline that combines LLM-driven query expansion, \textbf{MARVEL-Retriever} -- a reasoning-enhanced dense retriever fine-tuned for complex multimodal queries -- and GPT-4o-based chain-of-thought reranking with optional multi-pass reciprocal rank fusion. Evaluated on MM-BRIGHT across 29 technical domains, MARVEL achieves \textbf{37.9} nDCG@10, surpassing the best multimodal encoder by \textbf{+10.3 points} and outperforming all single-stage baselines in 27 of 29 domains and matching or approaching the best baseline in the remaining two highly-specialized domains (Crypto, Quantum Computing), demonstrating that reasoning-intensive multimodal retrieval is best addressed through a unified expand-retrieve-rerank framework. https://github.com/mm-bright/multimodal-reasoning-retrieval

</details>


### [175] [Leveraging Artist Catalogs for Cold-Start Music Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07090)
*Yan-Martin Tamm,Gregor Meehan,Vojtěch Nekl,Vojtěch Vančura,Rodrigo Alves,Johan Pauwels,Anna Aljanaki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文针对音乐推荐中的冷启动问题，提出利用艺术家层面的协同过滤信号来预测新曲目的用户偏好。核心创新是将"半冷启动"问题框架化，并提出ACARec模型，通过注意力机制聚合艺术家现有曲目的CF嵌入来生成新曲目的嵌入。实验表明，该方法相比纯内容基线在Recall和NDCG上提升超过一倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐推荐系统中，新曲目缺乏用户交互历史，传统协同过滤无法处理。现有方法虽利用内容特征（音频、文本、元数据）映射到CF空间，但通常忽略或简单处理艺术家信息，未能捕捉艺术家-曲目的层级结构。由于大多数新曲目来自有历史记录的艺术家，艺术家层面的丰富协同信号被浪费。

Method: 1) 提出"半冷启动"问题框架，将艺术家层级信号纳入冷启动问题；2) 提出ACARec模型，使用基于注意力机制的架构，通过聚合艺术家现有曲目的CF嵌入来生成新曲目的CF嵌入；3) 利用艺术家已有的协同过滤信号而非仅依赖内容特征。

Result: 艺术家感知方法相比纯内容基线在Recall和NDCG指标上提升超过100%；ACARec在新艺术家发现和冷启动曲目流行度预测方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 通过挖掘艺术家层面的协同信号可有效解决音乐冷启动推荐问题；艺术家-曲目层级关系的建模对于新曲目偏好预测具有重要意义；ACARec为冷启动场景提供了一种有效且具有实际应用价值的解决方案。

Abstract: The item cold-start problem poses a fundamental challenge for music recommendation: newly added tracks lack the interaction history that collaborative filtering (CF) requires. Existing approaches often address this problem by learning mappings from content features such as audio, text, and metadata to the CF latent space. However, previous works either omit artist information or treat it as just another input modality, missing the fundamental hierarchy of artists and items. Since most new tracks come from artists with previous history available, we frame cold-start track recommendation as 'semi-cold' by leveraging the rich collaborative signal that exists at the artist level. We show that artist-aware methods can more than double Recall and NDCG compared to content-only baselines, and propose ACARec, an attention-based architecture that generates CF embeddings for new tracks by attending over the artist's existing catalog. We show that our approach has notable advantages in predicting user preferences for new tracks, especially for new artist discovery and more accurate estimation of cold item popularity.

</details>


### [176] [BRIDGE: Multimodal-to-Text Retrieval via Reinforcement-Learned Query Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07201)
*Mohamed Darwish Mounis,Mohamed Mahmoud,Shaimaa Sedek,Mahmoud Abdalla,Mahmoud SalahEldin Kasem,Abdelrahman Abdallah,Hyun-Soo Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BRIDGE系统通过查询对齐策略解决多模态到文本检索的瓶颈问题。该系统包含两个组件：FORGE（基于强化学习训练的查询生成模型）和LENS（推理增强的密集检索器）。在MM-BRIGHT基准测试中，BRIDGE达到29.7 nDCG@10，将Nomic-Vision作为插件使用时达到33.3 nDCG@10，首次超越最佳纯文本检索器（32.2）。研究证明查询对齐是多模态到文本检索的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态检索系统在处理图像-文本查询时表现不佳，最好的视觉-语言编码器在MM-BRIGHT上仅达到27.6 nDCG@10，甚至不如纯文本检索器。作者认为瓶颈不在检索器本身，而在于查询——原始多模态查询混合了视觉描述、对话噪声和检索意图，导致嵌入相似度系统性下降。

Method: 提出BRIDGE两组件系统：(1) FORGE（Focused Retrieval Query Generator）：通过强化学习训练的查询对齐模型，将嘈杂的多模态查询精炼为紧凑、检索优化的搜索字符串；(2) LENS（Language-Enhanced Neural Search）：在推理密集型检索数据上微调的推理增强密集检索器，用于处理意图丰富的查询。

Result: 在MM-BRIGHT基准（2803个查询，29个领域）上，BRIDGE达到29.7 nDCG@10，超越所有多模态编码器基线（包括Nomic-Vision的27.6）。当FORGE作为即插即用对齐器应用于Nomic-Vision时，组合系统达到33.3 nDCG@10，超越最佳纯文本检索器（32.2）。

Conclusion: 查询对齐是多模态到文本检索的关键瓶颈。通过将嘈杂的多模态查询转换为优化的检索查询，系统可以在不依赖多模态编码器的情况下超越纯多模态方法，且组件具有可插拔性和可组合性。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval systems struggle to resolve image-text queries against text-only corpora: the best vision-language encoder achieves only 27.6 nDCG@10 on MM-BRIGHT, underperforming strong text-only retrievers. We argue the bottleneck is not the retriever but the query -- raw multimodal queries entangle visual descriptions, conversational noise, and retrieval intent in ways that systematically degrade embedding similarity. We present \textbf{BRIDGE}, a two-component system that resolves this mismatch without multimodal encoders. \textbf{FORGE} (\textbf{F}ocused Retrieval Query Generato\textbf{r}) is a query alignment model trained via reinforcement learning, which distills noisy multimodal queries into compact, retrieval-optimized search strings. \textbf{LENS} (\textbf{L}anguage-\textbf{E}nhanced \textbf{N}eural \textbf{S}earch) is a reasoning-enhanced dense retriever fine-tuned on reasoning-intensive retrieval data to handle the intent-rich queries FORGE produces. Evaluated on MM-BRIGHT (2,803 queries, 29 domains), BRIDGE achieves \textbf{29.7} nDCG@10, surpassing all multimodal encoder baselines including Nomic-Vision (27.6). When FORGE is applied as a plug-and-play aligner on top of Nomic-Vision, the combined system reaches \textbf{33.3} nDCG@10 -- exceeding the best text-only retriever (32.2) -- demonstrating that \textit{query alignment} is the key bottleneck in multimodal-to-text retrieval. https://github.com/mm-bright/multimodal-reasoning-retrieval

</details>


### [177] [HIVE: Query, Hypothesize, Verify An LLM Framework for Multimodal Reasoning-Intensive Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07220)
*Mahmoud Abdalla,Mahmoud SalahEldin Kasem,Mohamed Mahmoud,Mostafa Farouk Senussi,Abdelrahman Abdallah,Hyun-Soo Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HIVE框架，通过LLM驱动的假设生成和验证来解决多模态检索中视觉推理不足的问题，在MM-BRIGHT基准上达到41.7 nDCG@10，超越最佳文本检索模型(+9.5点)和最佳多模态模型(+14.1点)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态检索模型在需要深度整合图像与文本进行推理的查询上表现不佳，最佳多模态模型在MM-BRIGHT上仅达到27.6 nDCG@10，甚至不如强文本-only检索器(32.2)，存在明显的"多模态推理鸿沟"。

Method: HIVE框架采用四阶段流程：(1)语料库初检；(2)基于LLM的补偿性查询合成，明确阐述候选文档中观察到的视觉和逻辑gap；(3)使用精炼查询进行二次检索；(4)LLM验证和对候选并集进行重排序。该框架为即插即用设计，兼容标准检索器和推理增强型检索器。

Result: 在MM-BRIGHT的2,803个真实查询(29个技术领域)上，HIVE达到41.7 nDCG@10的最优结果。推理增强型基础检索器贡献33.2，HIVE框架额外增加8.5点。在视觉需求高的领域效果显著：游戏68.2，化学42.5，可持续性49.4。

Conclusion: LLM中介的视觉假设生成和验证可显著缩小检索中的多模态推理差距，证明显式视觉-文本推理注入是提升多模态检索的有效途径。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval models fail on reasoning-intensive queries where images (diagrams, charts, screenshots) must be deeply integrated with text to identify relevant documents -- the best multimodal model achieves only 27.6 nDCG@10 on MM-BRIGHT, underperforming even strong text-only retrievers (32.2). We introduce \textbf{HIVE} (\textbf{H}ypothesis-driven \textbf{I}terative \textbf{V}isual \textbf{E}vidence Retrieval), a plug-and-play framework that injects explicit visual-text reasoning into a retriever via LLMs. HIVE operates in four stages: (1) initial retrieval over the corpus, (2) LLM-based compensatory query synthesis that explicitly articulates visual and logical gaps observed in top-$k$ candidates, (3) secondary retrieval with the refined query, and (4) LLM verification and reranking over the union of candidates. Evaluated on the multimodal-to-text track of MM-BRIGHT (2,803 real-world queries across 29 technical domains), HIVE achieves a new state-of-the-art aggregated nDCG@10 of \textbf{41.7} -- a \textbf{+9.5} point gain over the best text-only model (DiVeR: 32.2) and \textbf{+14.1} over the best multimodal model (Nomic-Vision: 27.6), where our reasoning-enhanced base retriever contributes 33.2 and the HIVE framework adds a further \textbf{+8.5} points -- with particularly strong results in visually demanding domains (Gaming: 68.2, Chemistry: 42.5, Sustainability: 49.4). Compatible with both standard and reasoning-enhanced retrievers, HIVE demonstrates that LLM-mediated visual hypothesis generation and verification can substantially close the multimodal reasoning gap in retrieval. https://github.com/mm-bright/multimodal-reasoning-retrieval

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Code Sharing In Prediction Model Research: A Scoping Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06212)
*Thomas Sounack,Raffaele Giancotti,Catherine A. Gao,Lasai Barreñada,Hyeonhoon Lee,Hyung-Chul Lee,Leo Anthony Celi,Karel G. M. Moons,Gary S. Collins,Charlotta Lindvall,Tom Pollard*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过系统检索PubMed中引用TRIPOD声明的文章，评估预测模型研究中的代码共享现状。使用LLM辅助筛选和评估，发现仅12.2%的文章提供代码共享声明，且代码质量参差不齐，为TRIPOD-Code指南的制定提供实证依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测模型的诊断和预后研究代码可得性有限，尽管TRIPOD声明规范了预测模型报告标准，但未对代码仓库结构和文档做出明确规定。因此需要量化当前代码共享实践，为开发TRIPOD-Code扩展指南提供依据。

Method: 系统检索截至2025年8月11日引用TRIPOD或TRIPOD+AI的PubMed文章，限定为可通过PubMed Central开放获取API检索的研究。纳入标准为开发、更新或验证多变量预测模型的研究。采用LLM辅助pipeline进行文章筛选、代码可用性声明和仓库链接提取，并依据14项可复现性相关特征评估仓库。

Result: 在3967篇符合纳入标准的文章中，12.2%包含代码共享声明。代码共享比例随时间递增，2025年达15.8%。TRIPOD+AI引用研究的代码共享率高于TRIPOD引用研究。期刊和国家间存在显著差异。仓库评估显示可复现性特征异质性较大：多数仓库包含README文件(80.5%)，但指定依赖项的较少(37.6%，其中版本约束仅21.6%)，模块化仓库仅占42.4%。

Conclusion: 预测模型研究中代码共享仍不普遍，且即便共享也往往难以复用。研究结果为TRIPOD-Code扩展提供了实证基线，强调需要制定超越代码可用性的更明确规范，包括文档、依赖项规范、开源许可和可执行结构等方面。

Abstract: Analytical code is essential for reproducing diagnostic and prognostic prediction model research, yet code availability in the published literature remains limited. While the TRIPOD statements set standards for reporting prediction model methods, they do not define explicit standards for repository structure and documentation. This review quantifies current code-sharing practices to inform the development of TRIPOD-Code, a TRIPOD extension reporting guideline focused on code sharing.
  We conducted a scoping review of PubMed-indexed articles citing TRIPOD or TRIPOD+AI as of Aug 11, 2025, restricted to studies retrievable via the PubMed Central Open Access API. Eligible studies developed, updated, or validated multivariable prediction models. A large language model-assisted pipeline was developed to screen articles and extract code availability statements and repository links. Repositories were assessed with the same LLM against 14 predefined reproducibility-related features. Our code is made publicly available.
  Among 3,967 eligible articles, 12.2% included code sharing statements. Code sharing increased over time, reaching 15.8% in 2025, and was higher among TRIPOD+AI-citing studies than TRIPOD-citing studies. Sharing prevalence varied widely by journal and country. Repository assessment showed substantial heterogeneity in reproducibility features: most repositories contained a README file (80.5%), but fewer specified dependencies (37.6%; version-constrained 21.6%) or were modular (42.4%).
  In prediction model research, code sharing remains relatively uncommon, and when shared, often falls short of being reusable. These findings provide an empirical baseline for the TRIPOD-Code extension and underscore the need for clearer expectations beyond code availability, including documentation, dependency specification, licensing, and executable structure.

</details>


### [179] [All LCA models are wrong. Are some of them useful? Towards open computational LCA in ICT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06290)
*Vincent Corlay,David Bekri,Marie-Anne Lacroix,Maxime Pelcat,Maxime Peralta,Pierre-Yves Pichon,Leo Saillenfest,Olivier Weppe,Sebastien Rumley*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对ICT系统的LCA评估方法论进行批判性分析，指出当前实践中存在模型滥用、数据库选择、范围不匹配、聚合不透明和模型集成等问题，并提出通过显式依赖图、版本化模型库、完整性约束自动执行和模型分类学四项关键要求来提升ICT LCA的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ICT系统环境影响的量化需求增加，LCA方法被广泛采用，但ICT LCA本质上是一套模型系统，当前实践缺乏必要的严谨性，导致评估结果可信度存疑。

Method: 本文首先重温LCA基础理论，强调ICT LCA形成模型系统需要高度谨慎的构建、校准、集成和解释；然后通过典型案例分析文档化当前实践中的不足，包括数据库选择、范围不匹配、不透明聚合和模型集成等结构性挑战。

Result: 识别出可信ICT LCA的四个关键要求：明确的模型谱系（explicit model lineage）、清晰定义的模型范围（clearly defined model scope）、端到端可追溯性（end-to-end traceability）和管理的非过时性（managed non-obsolescence）。

Conclusion: 提出一个操作性框架，通过显式依赖图、开源版本化LCA导向模型库、完整性约束自动执行和明确定义的模型分类学来落实上述四项要求，旨在提升ICT LCA方法的科学严谨性和结果可信度。

Abstract: Life Cycle Assessment (LCA) is increasingly used to quantify and regulate the environmental impacts of Information and Communication Technology (ICT) systems. Since direct biosphere measurements are complicated to perform, we claim that the environmental impact assessment of ICT relies heavily on models. In this paper, we first revisit the fundamentals of LCA: we emphasize that ICT LCAs effectively form systems of models, and we argue that such systems require an extra-high level of carefulness in construction, calibration, integration, and interpretation. We then document how this level of rigor is challenging to achieve with current practices. This is illustrated with emblematic examples of model misuse and an analysis of structural challenges related to database choice, scope mismatches, opaque aggregation, and model integration. From this analysis, we derive four key requirements for credible ICT LCA: explicit model lineage, clearly defined model scope, end-to-end traceability, and managed non-obsolescence. Finally, we propose a framework that operationalizes these requirements using explicit dependency graphs, an open and versioned LCA-oriented model repository, automatic enforcement of integrity constraints, and a well-defined model taxonomy.

</details>


### [180] ["Don't Be Afraid, Just Learn": Insights from Industry Practitioners to Prepare Software Engineers in the Age of Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06342)
*Daniel Otten,Trevor Stalnaker,Nathan Wintersgill,Oscar Chaparro,Denys Poshyvanyk,Douglas Schmidt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Although tension between university curricula and industry expectations has existed in some form for decades, the rapid integration of generative AI (GenAI) tools into software development has recently widened the gap between the two domains. To better understand this disconnect, we surveyed 51 industry practitioners (software developers, technical leads, upper management, \etc) and conducted 11 follow-up interviews focused on hiring practices, required job skills, perceived shortcomings in university curricula, and views on how university learning outcomes can be improved. Our results suggest that GenAI creates demand for new skills (\eg prompting and output evaluation), while strengthening the importance of soft-skills (\eg problem solving and critical thinking) and traditional competencies (\eg architecture design and debugging). We synthesize these findings into actionable recommendations for academia (\eg how to incorporate GenAI into curricula and evaluation redesign). Our work offers empirical guidance to help educators prepare students for modern software engineering environments.

</details>


### [181] [A Survey of Algorithm Debt in Machine and Deep Learning Systems: Definition, Smells, and Future Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06363)
*Emmanuel Iko-Ojo Simon,Chirath Hettiarachchi,Fatemeh Fard,Alex Potanin,Hanna Suominen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一项关于机器学习/深度学习系统中"算法债务"(Algorithm Debt, AD)的系统文献综述，通过分析42项主要研究扩展了AD的定义，揭示了其隐性存在，识别了其"代码异味"(smells)，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ML/DL技术的广泛采用，系统维护挑战日益突出。算法债务作为一种技术债务类型，对ML/DL系统的性能和可扩展性产生严重影响，但目前缺乏对其系统性的研究和理解。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，对42项主要研究进行了综合分析。

Result: 研究结果包括：1) 扩展了AD的定义；2) 揭示了AD在ML/DL系统中的隐性存在；3) 识别了AD的相关代码异味；4) 提出了未来的研究方向。

Conclusion: 这些发现将指导后续以AD为重点的研究工作，最终提升ML/DL系统的可靠性。

Abstract: The adoption of Machine and Deep Learning (ML/DL) technologies introduces maintenance challenges, leading to Technical Debt (TD). Algorithm Debt (AD) is a TD type that impacts the performance and scalability of ML/DL systems. A review of 42 primary studies expanded AD's definition, uncovered its implicit presence, identified its smells, and highlighted future directions. These findings will guide an AD-focused study, enhancing the reliability of ML/DL systems.

</details>


### [182] [Beyond Functional Correctness: Design Issues in AI IDE-Generated Large-Scale Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06373)
*Syed Mohammad Kashif,Ruiyin Li,Peng Liang,Amjed Tahir,Qiong Feng,Zengyang Li,Mojtaba Shahin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究探索了 Cursor AI IDE 生成大规模软件项目的能力及其代码设计质量。采用特征驱动人机协作框架（FD-HITL）生成10个项目，平均功能正确性达91%，平均规模16,965行代码和114个文件。但静态分析发现大量设计问题，包括代码重复、高复杂度、方法过大等，违反SRP、SoC、DRY等设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新一代AI编程工具（如具备Agent能力的AI IDE）被认为能大规模生成项目级代码，但缺乏实证研究验证其在生成大型软件系统方面的实际能力以及生成代码的设计质量。

Method: 研究提出FD-HITL框架指导项目生成，在3个应用领域和多种技术栈中使用Cursor生成了10个项目；通过人工评估功能正确性，使用CodeScene和SonarQube两个静态分析工具检测设计问题。

Result: 生成的10个项目平均规模达16,965行代码和114个文件，功能正确性平均得分为91%。CodeScene检测到1,305个设计问题（9类），SonarQube检测到3,193个问题（11类）。主要问题包括：代码重复、高代码复杂度、方法过大、框架最佳实践违规、异常处理问题和可访问性问题。

Conclusion: Cursor配合FD-HITL框架能生成功能完整的大规模项目，但生成的代码存在维护性和可演进性风险设计问题，违反SRP（单一职责原则）、SoC（关注点分离）和DRY（不要重复自己）原则，需有经验开发者仔细审查。

Abstract: New generation of AI coding tools, including AI-powered IDEs equipped with agentic capabilities, can generate code within the context of the project. These AI IDEs are increasingly perceived as capable of producing project-level code at scale. However, there is limited empirical evidence on the extent to which they can generate large-scale software systems and what design issues such systems may exhibit. To address this gap, we conducted a study to explore the capability of Cursor in generating large-scale projects and to evaluate the design quality of projects generated by Cursor. First, we propose a Feature-Driven Human-In-The-Loop (FD-HITL) framework that systematically guides project generation from curated project descriptions. We generated 10 projects using Cursor with the FD-HITL framework across three application domains and multiple technologies. We assessed the functional correctness of these projects through manual evaluation, obtaining an average functional correctness score of 91%. Next, we analyzed the generated projects using two static analysis tools, CodeScene and SonarQube, to detect design issues. We identified 1,305 design issues categorized into 9 categories by CodeScene and 3,193 issues in 11 categories by SonarQube. Our findings show that (1) when used with the FD-HITL framework, Cursor can generate functional large-scale projects averaging 16,965 LoC and 114 files; (2) the generated projects nevertheless contain design issues that may pose long-term maintainability and evolvability risks, requiring careful review by experienced developers; (3) the most prevalent issues include Code Duplication, high Code Complexity, Large Methods, Framework Best-Practice Violations, Exception-Handling Issues and Accessibility Issues; (4) these design issues violate design principles such as SRP, SoC, and DRY. The replication package is at https://github.com/Kashifraz/DIinAGP

</details>


### [183] [ExplainFuzz: Explainable and Constraint-Conditioned Test Generation with Probabilistic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06559)
*Annaëlle Baiget,Jaron Maene,Seongmin Lee,Benjie Wang,Guy Van den Broeck,Miryung Kim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ExplainFuzz是一个利用概率电路(PCs)学习并查询CFG生成测试输入结构化分布的测试生成框架，通过条件化能力实现约束感知采样，在SQL和XML测试中显著提升bug触发率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试生成方法（基于语法的模糊器、概率CFG、大语言模型）存在关键局限：常产生不合语法的输入、无法反映真实数据分布、难以捕获上下文敏感的依赖关系、缺乏可解释性。

Method: ExplainFuzz从CFG出发，编译语法感知PC并在现有输入上训练，通过采样生成新输入；利用PC的条件化能力融入测试约束（如查询必须包含GROUP BY），实现约束感知的概率采样。

Result: 相比pCFG、语法无关PC和LLM，ExplainFuzz显著降低困惑度，提升约束满足输入的多样性；相比语法感知变异模糊，SQL bug触发率从35%升至63%，XML从10%升至100%。

Conclusion: 概率电路有潜力作为语法感知、可控测试生成的基础，能捕获上下文敏感的依赖关系，证明了学习型输入分布优于依赖种子输入局部探索的变异模糊方法。

Abstract: Understanding and explaining the structure of generated test inputs is essential for effective software testing and debugging. Existing approaches--including grammar-based fuzzers, probabilistic Context-Free Grammars (pCFGs), and Large Language Models (LLMs)--suffer from critical limitations. They frequently produce ill-formed inputs that fail to reflect realistic data distributions, struggle to capture context-sensitive probabilistic dependencies, and lack explainability. We introduce ExplainFuzz, a test generation framework that leverages Probabilistic Circuits (PCs) to learn and query structured distributions over grammar-based test inputs interpretably and controllably. Starting from a Context-Free Grammar (CFG), ExplainFuzz compiles a grammar-aware PC and trains it on existing inputs. New inputs are then generated via sampling. ExplainFuzz utilizes the conditioning capability of PCs to incorporate test-specific constraints (e.g., a query must have GROUP BY), enabling constrained probabilistic sampling to generate inputs satisfying grammar and user-provided constraints.
  Our results show that ExplainFuzz improves the coherence and realism of generated inputs, achieving significant perplexity reduction compared to pCFGs, grammar-unaware PCs, and LLMs. By leveraging its native conditioning capability, ExplainFuzz significantly enhances the diversity of inputs that satisfy a user-provided constraint. Compared to grammar-aware mutational fuzzing, ExplainFuzz increases bug-triggering rates from 35% to 63% in SQL and from 10% to 100% in XML. These results demonstrate the power of a learned input distribution over mutational fuzzing, which is often limited to exploring the local neighborhood of seed inputs. These capabilities highlight the potential of PCs to serve as a foundation for grammar-aware, controllable test generation that captures context-sensitive, probabilistic dependencies.

</details>


### [184] [It's Not About Whom You Train: An Analysis of Corporate Education in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06580)
*Rodrigo Siqueira,Danilo Monteiro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一项针对282名巴西软件工程专业人员的调查研究，探索社会人口统计学和专业变量对企业培训质量和有效性感知的影响。研究发现，强制培训是主要影响因素（24/27项），而个人特征变量无显著差异，专业轨迹变量产生局部差异，性别无差异表明障碍发生在培训之前。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业培训是软件行业的战略投资，但学界对不同专业群体如何感知这些培训举措知之甚少。研究旨在填补这一空白，探究社会人口统计学和专业变量是否影响软件工程领域企业培训的感知质量。

Method: 对282名巴西专业人员开展问卷调查，应用非参数显著性检验，将27项感知条目与9个社会人口统计学变量（性别、年龄、教育程度、州、工作经验、专业级别、公司规模、工作领域、参与性质）进行交叉分析，共计243种组合。

Result: 243种组合中仅35种显示统计显著性。培训强制性是最主要影响因素（影响24/27项）；工作经验呈非线性模式，3-6年出现低参与区；工作领域差异表明高级技术角色的软技能培训存在显著差距；个人特征变量和公司规模未产生相关显著差异。

Conclusion: 个人特征变量不能决定培训质量和有效性的感知，而专业轨迹变量（经验、级别、工作领域）产生局部差异。参与的自愿性是关键决定因素。性别无差异（样本含23%女性）表明障碍存在于培训之前——体现在准入和代表性层面，而非学习体验过程中。

Abstract: Context: Corporate education is a strategic investment in the software industry, but little is known about how different professional profiles perceive these initiatives. Objective: To investigate whether sociodemographic and professional variables influence the perception of quality and effectiveness of corporate training in Software Engineering (SE). Method: Non-parametric significance tests were applied to data from a survey with 282 Brazilian professionals, crossing 27 perception items with 9 sociodemographic variables (gender, age, education level, state, experience, professional level, company size, area of work, and nature of participation), totaling 243 combinations. Results: Of the 243 combinations tested, only 35 showed statistical significance. Training mandatoriness was the dominant factor, affecting 24 of 27 items. Length of experience revealed a non-linear descriptive pattern with a low-engagement zone between 3 and 6 years. Differences by area of work indicated an expressive gap in soft skills training for advanced technical roles. Personal profile variables and company size produced no relevant significant differences. Conclusion: Personal profile variables do not determine the perception of quality and effectiveness, while professional trajectory variables (experience, level, area of work) produce localized differences. The voluntariness of participation remains a determining factor, in line with the literature. The absence of gender differences in a sample with 23\% women suggests that barriers operate before training, in access and representation, not during the learning experience.

</details>


### [185] [Benchmarking Requirement-to-Architecture Generation with Hybrid Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06683)
*Minxiao Li,Shuying Yan,Li Zhang,Yang Liu,Fang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: R2ABench是一个用于评估LLM从需求文档生成软件架构图能力的新型基准测试，配套多维度混合评估框架，并揭示了当前模型在关系推理方面的核心缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏针对从需求文档生成软件架构设计这一任务的功能性数据集，制约了该领域的研究与发展。

Method: 提出R2ABench基准（含真实软件项目、PRD文档及专家标注的PlantUML图表）和三层评估框架（结构图指标、多维度评分、架构反模式检测），对主流模型和Agent工作流进行实证评估。

Result: LLM在句法有效性和实体提取方面表现强健，但在关系推理上存在根本性缺陷，导致架构结构碎片化；代码专用模型部分缓解该问题；Agent框架引入显著不稳定性而非稳定提升。

Conclusion: R2ABench为推进LLM驱动的软件架构生成研究提供了稳健且标准化的评估基础。

Abstract: Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated significant potential in automating software engineering tasks. Generating software architecture designs from requirement documents is a crucial step in software development. However, there is currently a lack of functional datasets tailored for this task. To bridge this gap, we introduce R2ABench (Requirement-To-Architecture Benchmark), a novel benchmark comprising diverse real-world software projects paired with comprehensive Product Requirements Documents (PRDs) and expert-curated PlantUML reference diagrams. Furthermore, we propose a multi-dimensional, hybrid evaluation framework that assesses generated diagrams across three complementary layers: Structural Graph Metrics, Multi-dimensional Scoring, and Architecture Anti-pattern Detection. Using this framework, we conducted a comprehensive empirical study evaluating state-of-the-art models and agentic workflows. Our study shows that LLMs show strong syntactic validity and robust entity extraction but fundamentally struggle with relational reasoning, leading to structurally fragmented architectures. Code-specialized models partially alleviate this limitation, while agent frameworks introduce significant instability rather than consistent improvements. R2ABench provides a robust and standardized foundation for advancing LLM-driven software architecture generation.

</details>


### [186] [Stabilization Without Simplification: A Two-Dimensional Model of Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06709)
*Masaru Furukawa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于图论的离散时间概率框架，将软件演化中的结构性负担与不确定性分离。该框架证明，在特定假设下，存在一种"稳定化而不简化"的 regime，即系统的可预测性提高而结构性负担不降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件系统规模、复杂度和相互依赖性持续增长，但许多大型系统仍保持稳定。传统的一维演化视角无法解释这一现象——系统如何在负担增加时仍保持稳定？这表明需要新的理论框架来理解软件演化。

Method: 提出了基于图的离散时间概率框架，将变更工作建模为随机变量，由依赖邻域和残余变异性决定。框架中：负担(burden)定义为工作量的期望值，不确定性(uncertainty)定义为工作量的方差。在非递减平均结构负载、结构正则化、过程稳定化和协方差控制等显式假设下进行分析。

Result: 证明了在上述假设条件下，存在一个 regime 使得不确定性降低而结构性负担不降低。这一结果形式化地描述了"稳定化而不简化"(stabilization without simplification)现象。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解软件系统如何随时间变得更加可预测而无需结构简化提供了最小化理论解释，并为软件演化的进一步理论和实证研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Software systems are widely observed to grow in size, complexity, and interdependence over time, yet many large-scale systems remain stable despite persistent structural burden. This apparent tension suggests a limitation in one-dimensional views of software evolution. This paper introduces a graph-based, discrete-time probabilistic framework that separates structural burden from uncertainty. Change effort is modeled as a stochastic variable determined by the dependency neighborhood of the changed entity and by residual variability. Within this framework, burden is defined as expected effort and uncertainty as variance of effort. We show that, under explicit assumptions on non-decreasing average structural load, structural regularization, process stabilization, and covariance control, there exists a regime in which uncertainty decreases while structural burden does not. This regime formalizes the phenomenon of stabilization without simplification. The proposed framework provides a minimal theoretical explanation for how software systems can become more predictable over time without necessarily becoming structurally simpler, and offers a foundation for further theoretical and empirical studies of software evolution.

</details>


### [187] [Fine-grained Approaches for Confidence Calibration of LLMs in Automated Code Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06723)
*Hong Yi Lin,Chunhua Liu,Haoyu Gao,Patanamon Thongtanunam,Christoph Treude*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对自动化代码修订(ACR)任务中LLM置信度校准不准确的问题，提出局部Platt缩放方法，对细粒度置信度分数进行独立校准。实验证明该方法在14种模型、3个任务上均能显著降低校准误差，为LLM在代码修复等任务中的可信应用提供了实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在代码修复、漏洞修复和代码优化等自动化代码修订(ACR)任务中存在输出不完美的问题，而现有的全局Platt缩放方法由于粒度过粗，无法有效校准这些任务的置信度分数，导致开发者难以判断模型输出的可靠性。

Method: 提出局部Platt缩放方法，对三类细粒度置信度分数分别独立应用Platt缩放进行校准：(1)对每个样本/任务的置信度独立校准；(2)将细粒度校准与全局Platt缩放相结合形成增强方案。

Result: 在14种不同规模模型、3个ACR任务及多种正确性度量指标上，细粒度置信度分数在更广泛概率区间内一致实现更低校准误差；结合全局Platt缩放后效果进一步增强。

Conclusion: 细粒度置信度校准方法为ACR任务提供了实用、可行的置信度校准方案，使开发者能够更可靠地判断模型输出的可信度，提升了LLM在代码工程任务中的实用性和信任度。

Abstract: In today's AI-assisted software engineering landscape, developers increasingly depend on LLMs that are highly capable, yet inherently imperfect. The tendency of these models to produce incorrect outputs can reduce developer productivity. To this end, a canonical mitigation method is to provide calibrated confidence scores that faithfully reflect their likelihood of correctness at the instance-level. Such information allows users to make immediate decisions regarding output acceptance, abstain error-prone outputs, and better align their expectations with the model's capabilities. Since post-trained LLMs do not inherently produce well-calibrated confidence scores, researchers have developed post-hoc calibration methods, with global Platt-scaling of sequence-level confidence scores proving effective in many generative software engineering tasks but remaining unreliable or unexplored for automated code revision (ACR) tasks such as program repair, vulnerability repair, and code refinement. We hypothesise that the coarse-grained nature of this conventional method makes it ill-suited for ACR tasks, where correctness is often determined by local edit decisions and miscalibration can be sample-dependent, thereby motivating fine-grained confidence calibration. To address this, our study proposes local Platt-scaling applied separately to three different fine-grained confidence scores. Through experiments across 3 separate tasks and correctness metrics, as well as 14 different models of various sizes, we find that fine-grained confidence scores consistently achieve lower calibration error across a broader range of probability intervals, and this effect is further amplified when global Platt-scaling is applied. Our proposed approaches offer a practical solution to eliciting well-calibrated confidence scores, enabling more trustworthy and streamlined usage of imperfect models in ACR tasks.

</details>


### [188] [Babbling Suppression: Making LLMs Greener One Token at a Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06755)
*Lola Solovyeva,Fernando Castor*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Babbling Suppression (BS)方法，通过在LLM代码生成过程中集成测试执行，在解决方案通过所有测试时终止生成，从而减少不必要的token输出。实验表明该方法可将Python和Java的能源消耗分别降低最多65%和62%，具有模型无关性，可广泛推广应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLM)在辅助代码生成、补全和重构时经常产生冗余输出("babbling")，导致额外的认知、经济和能源成本。

Method: 提出Babbling Suppression (BS)方法，将测试执行集成到LLM生成过程中，评估中间输出并在解决方案通过所有测试时终止生成。在两个Python和两个Java基准测试上，对四个3-4B参数模型和六个6-7B参数模型进行了实证研究。

Result: Babbling在所有测试模型中均有出现，Java中发生频率高于Python。应用BS后，Python能源消耗降低最多65%，Java降低最多62%。在40个模型-基准测试对中，29个显示平均能源消耗降低，22个降低超过20%；35个的生成token数减少；26个的BS GPU能源开销低于10%，最高仅24%。

Conclusion: BS通过减少能源消耗和开发者认知负担，使AI辅助编程更加高效和可持续。其模型无关的设计便于集成，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Context: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in modern software development, aiding in code generation, code completion, and refactoring through AI-powered assistants. While they accelerate development workflows, they often produce extraneous output, referred to as "babbling", which incurs additional cognitive, economic, and energy costs. Objective: This work investigates the problem of babbling in LLM-based code generation and proposes a practical, model-agnostic approach to reduce unnecessary output without compromising solution accuracy. Method: We introduce Babbling Suppression (BS), a method that integrates test execution into the LLM generation process by evaluating intermediate outputs and terminating generation once a solution passes all tests. This prevents excessive token generation while having no impact on model accuracy. An empirical study was conducted across two Python and two Java benchmarks, targeting four 3-4B parameter models and six 6-7B parameter models. Results: Our findings show that babbling occurs across all tested models, with higher frequency in Java than in Python. Applying BS significantly reduces energy consumption by up to 65% for Python and 62% for Java in models prone to babbling. Across 40 model-benchmark pairs, 29 showed reduced mean energy consumption, with reductions exceeding 20% in 22 cases. Generated token count decreased in 35 pairs, while the GPU energy-per-token overhead of BS remained below 10% for 26 pairs, decreased for 2, and reached a maximum of 24%, yielding net energy savings in most cases. Implications: BS can make AI-assisted programming more efficient and sustainable by reducing energy consumption and minimizing cognitive effort by developers. Its model-agnostic design allows easy integration, suggesting broad applicability.

</details>


### [189] [Improving Random Testing via LLM-powered UI Tarpit Escaping for Mobile Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06763)
*Mengqian Xu,Yiheng Xiong,Le Chang,Ting Su,Chengcheng Wan,Weikai Miao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM驱动的随机GUI测试方法，通过监控UI相似度识别探索泥沼，并利用LLM建议逃脱事件来提升移动应用测试效果。在12个真实应用和WeChat上的实验表明，该方法可将覆盖率提升40%-55%，发现75个唯一Bug，其中34个为未知Bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机GUI测试存在UI探索泥沼问题，探索过程被困在局部UI区域，限制测试覆盖率和Bug发现能力。

Method: 提出混合测试方法：通过UI相似度监控识别泥沼，查询LLM建议有前景的逃脱事件。实现为HybridMonkey和HybridDroidbot两个工具。

Result: HybridMonkey和HybridDroidbot在12个真实应用中分别实现54.8%和44.8%的平均覆盖率提升，检测到75个唯一Bug（34个未知Bug），其中26个已确认修复。WeChat工业验证同样有效。

Conclusion: LLM辅助的随机GUI测试能有效缓解UI泥沼问题，显著提升测试覆盖率和Bug发现能力，兼具学术创新性和工业实用价值。

Abstract: Random GUI testing is a widely-used technique for testing mobile apps. However, its effectiveness is limited by the notorious issue -- UI exploration tarpits, where the exploration is trapped in local UI regions, thus impeding test coverage and bug discovery. In this experience paper, we introduce LLM-powered random GUI Testing, a novel hybrid testing approach to mitigating UI tarpits during random testing. Our approach monitors UI similarity to identify tarpits and query LLMs to suggest promising events for escaping the encountered tarpits. We implement our approach on top of two different automated input generation (AIG) tools for mobile apps: (1) HybridMonkey upon Monkey, a state-of-the-practice tool; and (2) HybridDroidbot upon Droidbot, a state-of-the-art tool. We evaluated them on 12 popular, real-world apps. The results show that HybridMonkey and HybridDroidbot outperform all baselines, achieving average coverage improvements of 54.8% and 44.8%, respectively, and detecting the highest number of unique crashes. In total, we found 75 unique bugs, including 34 previously unknown bugs. To date, 26 bugs have been confirmed and fixed. We also applied HybridMonkey on WeChat, a popular industrial app with billions of monthly active users. HybridMonkey achieved higher activity coverage and found more bugs than random testing.

</details>


### [190] [Evaluating Repository-level Software Documentation via Question Answering and Feature-Driven Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06793)
*Xinchen Wang,Ruida Hu,Cuiyun Gao,Pengfei Gao,Chao Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWD-Bench是一个用于评估仓库级软件文档质量的新基准测试。它创新性地通过功能驱动的问答任务来评估文档质量，而非直接打分。基准包含4,170条数据，通过挖掘高质量Pull Requests并补充仓库级上下文构建。最佳文档生成方法能使SWE-Agent的问题解决率提升20%，证明了高质量文档在支持文档驱动开发方面的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件文档基准测试存在两大局限：(1)缺乏整体性的仓库级评估；(2)依赖不可靠的评估策略（如LLM-as-a-judge），该方法存在评判标准模糊、仓库级知识不足等问题。

Method: 受文档驱动开发启发，提出通过评估LLM能否利用文档理解和实现功能来间接衡量文档质量。构建包含三个关联QA任务的SWD-Bench：(1)功能检测——判断功能是否被描述；(2)功能定位——评估定位相关文件的准确性；(3)功能补全——衡量实现细节的完整性。基准通过挖掘高质量Pull Requests并补充仓库级上下文构建。

Result: 基准包含4,170条数据。实验揭示现有文档生成方法的局限性，并发现源代码具有互补价值。最佳文档方法使SWE-Agent的问题解决率提升20%。

Conclusion: SWD-Bench通过功能驱动的QA任务提供了新颖的仓库级文档评估范式，验证了高质量文档在支持文档驱动开发中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Software documentation is crucial for repository comprehension. While Large Language Models (LLMs) advance documentation generation from code snippets to entire repositories, existing benchmarks have two key limitations: (1) they lack a holistic, repository-level assessment, and (2) they rely on unreliable evaluation strategies, such as LLM-as-a-judge, which suffers from vague criteria and limited repository-level knowledge. To address these issues, we introduce SWD-Bench, a novel benchmark for evaluating repository-level software documentation. Inspired by documentation-driven development, our strategy evaluates documentation quality by assessing an LLM's ability to understand and implement functionalities using the documentation, rather than by directly scoring it. This is measured through function-driven Question Answering (QA) tasks. SWD-Bench comprises three interconnected QA tasks: (1) Functionality Detection, to determine if a functionality is described; (2) Functionality Localization, to evaluate the accuracy of locating related files; and (3) Functionality Completion, to measure the comprehensiveness of implementation details. We construct the benchmark, containing 4,170 entries, by mining high-quality Pull Requests and enriching them with repository-level context. Experiments reveal limitations in current documentation generation methods and show that source code provides complementary value. Notably, documentation from the best-performing method improves the issue-solving rate of SWE-Agent by 20.00%, which demonstrates the practical value of high-quality documentation in supporting documentation-driven development.

</details>


### [191] [An empirical study of LoRA-based fine-tuning of large language models for automated test case generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06946)
*Milad Moradi,Ke Yan,David Colwell,Rhona Asgari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一项关于使用参数高效微调方法（LoRA）从自然语言需求自动生成测试用例的实证研究。实验表明，LoRA微调能显著提升开源模型性能，其中Ministral-8B表现最佳，且微调后的8B开源模型可媲美未微调的GPT-4.1模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言需求的歧义性以及生成结构化、可执行测试工件的需求，使得自动化测试用例生成成为软件工程中的挑战性问题。虽然LLM在此任务上展现出潜力，但其有效性依赖于任务特定的适配和高效的微调策略。

Method: 采用统一实验流程，评估多个LLM家族（包括开源和专有模型）；系统探索LoRA超参数（rank、scaling factor、dropout）的影响；提出基于GPT-4o的九维质量评估框架。

Result: LoRA微调显著提升所有开源模型性能；Ministral-8B在开源模型中表现最佳；微调后的8B开源模型性能可与未微调的GPT-4.1模型相当；GPT-4.1整体性能最高，但微调后开源与专有模型的性能差距大幅缩小。

Conclusion: 经过精心设计的微调方法，成本高效、可本地部署的开源模型可作为专有系统的可行替代方案。研究为自动化测试生成的模型选择、微调策略和评估方法提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Automated test case generation from natural language requirements remains a challenging problem in software engineering due to the ambiguity of requirements and the need to produce structured, executable test artifacts. Recent advances in LLMs have shown promise in addressing this task; however, their effectiveness depends on task-specific adaptation and efficient fine-tuning strategies. In this paper, we present a comprehensive empirical study on the use of parameter-efficient fine-tuning, specifically LoRA, for requirement-based test case generation. We evaluate multiple LLM families, including open-source and proprietary models, under a unified experimental pipeline. The study systematically explores the impact of key LoRA hyperparameters, including rank, scaling factor, and dropout, on downstream performance. We propose an automated evaluation framework based on GPT-4o, which assesses generated test cases across nine quality dimensions. Experimental results demonstrate that LoRA-based fine-tuning significantly improves the performance of all open-source models, with Ministral-8B achieving the best results among them. Furthermore, we show that a fine-tuned 8B open-source model can achieve performance comparable to pre-fine-tuned GPT-4.1 models, highlighting the effectiveness of parameter-efficient adaptation. While GPT-4.1 models achieve the highest overall performance, the performance gap between proprietary and open-source models is substantially reduced after fine-tuning. These findings provide important insights into model selection, fine-tuning strategies, and evaluation methods for automated test generation. In particular, they demonstrate that cost-efficient, locally deployable open-source models can serve as viable alternatives to proprietary systems when combined with well-designed fine-tuning approaches.

</details>


### [192] [Assessing REST API Test Generation Strategies with Log Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07073)
*Nana Reinikainen,Mika Mäntylä,Yuqing Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出基于日志覆盖率的三个指标来评估黑盒环境下REST API测试的有效性，并对比了进化计算(EvoMaster)、大语言模型(Claude Opus 4.6和GPT-5.2-Codex)及人工编写的Locust负载测试三种策略。实验表明Claude Opus 4.6表现最优，各策略间具有显著互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒环境下REST API测试有效性评估面临挑战：缺乏源代码覆盖率指标的访问权限，且技术栈多语言化（polyglot）增加了评估难度。现有方法难以全面衡量不同测试生成策略的效果。

Method: 提出三种日志覆盖率指标（平均值、最小值、最大值）来处理多次运行的多样化测试生成结果和运行时行为。在Light-OAuth2授权微服务系统上，对三种REST API测试生成策略进行实证评估：(1)进化计算(EvoMaster v5.0.2)；(2)大语言模型(Claude Opus 4.6和GPT-5.2-Codex)；(3)人工编写的Locust负载测试。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6测试比人工测试多发现28.4%的唯一日志模板，而EvoMaster和GPT-5.2-Codex分别少发现26.1%和38.6%。组合覆盖分析显示：人工测试+Claude Opus 4.6组合使总日志覆盖率提升78.4%和38.9%；Locust+EvoMaster组合提升30.7%和76.9%；Locust+GPT-5.2-Codex组合提升26.1%和105.6%。

Conclusion: 测试生成策略在运行时行为上表现出显著差异和互补性。单一策略无法覆盖所有测试场景，组合使用不同策略可大幅提升总体测试覆盖率。未来工作将扩展至多个系统进行验证。

Abstract: Assessing the effectiveness of REST API tests in black-box settings can be challenging due to the lack of access to source code coverage metrics and polyglot tech stack. We propose three metrics for capturing average, minimum, and maximum log coverage to handle the diverse test generation results and runtime behaviors over multiple runs. Using log coverage, we empirically evaluate three REST API test generation strategies, Evolutionary computing (EvoMaster v5.0.2), LLMs (Claude Opus 4.6 and GPT-5.2-Codex), and human-written Locust load tests, on Light-OAuth2 authorization microservice system. On average, Claude Opus 4.6 tests uncover 28.4% more unique log templates than human-written tests, whereas EvoMaster and GPT-5.2-Codex find 26.1% and 38.6% fewer, respectively. Next, we analyze combined log coverage to assess complementarity between strategies. Combining human-written tests with Claude Opus 4.6 tests increases total observed log coverage by 78.4% and 38.9% in human-written and Claude tests respectively. When combining Locust tests with EvoMaster the same increases are 30.7% and 76.9% and when using GPT-5.2-Codex 26.1% and 105.6%. This means that the generation strategies exercise largely distinct runtime behaviors. Our future work includes extending our study to multiple systems.

</details>


### [193] [Compact Constraint Encoding for LLM Code Generation: An Empirical Study of Token Economics and Constraint Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07192)
*Hanzhang Tang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了代码生成任务中紧凑结构化约束头是否能减少提示词令牌消耗，同时不降低约束合规率。实验覆盖11个模型、16个基准任务和830余次LLM调用，结果表明紧凑编码可减少约71%的约束相关令牌和25-30%的完整提示令牌，但未发现编码形式或传播模式对约束满足率(CSR)有显著影响。合规率的主要差异来源于约束类型（常规vs反直觉）和任务领域，而非编码格式。因此，紧凑约束编码的主要收益在于令牌节省而非合规提升，工程优化应聚焦于约束设计而非提示格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成任务中，工程约束通常以冗长自然语言形式表达，导致大量令牌消耗。研究者希望探究是否可以用紧凑结构化形式替代，从而节省令牌而不影响约束遵守效果。

Method: 进行了六轮实验，涵盖11个模型、16个基准任务和830+次LLM调用，测试三种编码形式和四种传播模式，采用规则基评分和模型自评估进行约束满足率对比，并通过Cliff's δ效应量分析统计显著性。

Result: 紧凑头可减少约束部分令牌约71%、完整提示令牌25-30%；编码形式和传播模式对CSR无显著影响(Cliff's δ < 0.01)；最大合规差异来源于约束类型（常规vs反直觉）和任务领域；模型自评估系统性高估合规率。

Conclusion: 紧凑约束编码的主要价值在于令牌节省而非合规改善；合规优化应优先关注约束设计本身而非提示格式；反直觉约束与模型默认的冲突是合规失败的主因，常规约束可达99%+合规率。

Abstract: LLMs used for code generation are typically guided by engineering constraints--technology choices, dependency restrictions, and architectural patterns--expressed in verbose natural language. We investigate whether compact, structured constraint headers can reduce prompt token consumption without degrading constraint compliance.
  Across six experimental rounds spanning 11 models, 16 benchmark tasks, and over 830 LLM invocations, we find that compact headers reduce constraint-portion tokens by approximately 71% and full-prompt tokens by 25--30%, replicated across three independent rounds. However, we detect no statistically significant differences in constraint satisfaction rate (CSR) across three encoding forms or four propagation modes; observed effect sizes are negligible (Cliff's $δ$ < 0.01, 95% CI spanning $\pm$2.6 percentage points). This null pattern holds across two models from different capability tiers. A supplementary experiment with four non-CSS tasks provides additional cross-domain support for the encoding null result.
  The largest observed sources of compliance variance are constraint type ($Δ$ = 9 percentage points between normal and counter-intuitive constraints) and task domain: counter-intuitive constraints opposing model defaults fail at 10--100%, while conventional constraints achieve 99%+ compliance regardless of encoding. Model self-assessments systematically overestimate compliance relative to rule-based scoring, revealing a gap between constraint understanding and execution. Under the tested conditions, the primary benefit of compact constraint encoding is token reduction rather than compliance improvement, and engineering effort toward compliance is better directed at constraint design than prompt formatting.

</details>


### [194] [Chatbot-Based Assessment of Code Understanding in Automated Programming Assessment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07304)
*Eduard Frankford,Erik Cikalleshi,Ruth Breu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM时代传统自动编程评估面临的挑战，提出了一套混合苏格拉底式验证框架。论文首先对编程教育中的会话式评估方法进行了饱和式范围审查，识别出三类主流架构（规则/模板驱动、LLM驱动、混合系统），并指出幻觉、过度依赖、隐私和诚信等问题。随后，该研究将这些发现综合为一种混合框架，结合确定性代码分析与双智能体会话层、知识追踪、支架式提问等技术，用于验证学生是否真正理解所提交的代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型使学生能够生成功能正确的代码而不必真正理解代码，导致传统的自动编程评估系统无法有效区分"真理解"与"假成果"。因此需要开发新的验证机制来判断学生是否真正掌握所提交的代码。

Method: 采用饱和式范围审查（saturation-based scoping review）方法，对编程教育中的会话式评估文献进行系统性梳理；随后基于综述发现，综合构建了混合苏格拉底式框架，整合确定性代码分析、双智能体对话层、知识追踪、支架式提问及防护栏等技术组件。

Result: 识别出三类主流架构体系：基于规则/模板的系统、基于LLM的系统、以及混合系统；提出了包含确定性代码分析、双智能体对话层、知识追踪、代码执行状态关联的推理验证、随机化追踪问题等组件的混合苏格拉底式框架；讨论了防范LLM生成解释的实践策略，包括监考式部署、随机追踪问题、本地模型部署等隐私保护方案。

Conclusion: 该框架并非替代传统测试，而是作为补充层用于验证学生是否真正理解所提交的代码；强调需要结合多种技术手段和制度设计来应对LLM在教育评估中带来的诚信与隐私挑战。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) challenge conventional automated programming assessment because students can now produce functionally correct code without demonstrating corresponding understanding. This paper makes two contributions. First, it reports a saturation-based scoping review of conversational assessment approaches in programming education. The review identifies three dominant architectural families: rule-based or template-driven systems, LLM-based systems, and hybrid systems. Across the literature, conversational agents appear promising for scalable feedback and deeper probing of code understanding, but important limitations remain around hallucinations, over-reliance, privacy, integrity, and deployment constraints. Second, the paper synthesizes these findings into a Hybrid Socratic Framework for integrating conversational verification into Automated Programming Assessment Systems (APASs). The framework combines deterministic code analysis with a dual-agent conversational layer, knowledge tracking, scaffolded questioning, and guardrails that tie prompts to runtime facts. The paper also discusses practical safeguards against LLM-generated explanations, including proctored deployment modes, randomized trace questions, stepwise reasoning tied to concrete execution states, and local-model deployment options for privacy-sensitive settings. Rather than replacing conventional testing, the framework is intended as a complementary layer for verifying whether students understand the code they submit.

</details>


### [195] [ReCodeAgent: A Multi-Agent Workflow for Language-agnostic Translation and Validation of Large-scale Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07341)
*Ali Reza Ibrahimzada,Brandon Paulsen,Daniel Kroening,Reyhaneh Jabbarvand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Most repository-level code translation and validation techniques have been evaluated on a single source-target programming language (PL) pair, owing to the complex engineering effort required to adapt new PL pairs. Programming agents can enable PL-agnosticism in repository-level code translation and validation: they can synthesize code across many PLs and autonomously use existing tools specific to each PL's analysis. However, state-of-the-art has yet to offer a fully autonomous agentic approach for repository-level code translation and validation of large-scale programs. This paper proposes ReCodeAgent, an autonomous multi-agent approach for language-agnostic repository-level code translation and validation. Users only need to provide the project in the source PL and specify the target PL for ReCodeAgent to automatically translate and validate the entire repository. ReCodeAgent is the first technique to achieve high translation success rates across many PLs.
  We compare the effectiveness of ReCodeAgent with four alternative neuro-symbolic and agentic approaches to translate 118 real-world projects, with 1,975 LoC and 43 translation units for each project, on average. The projects cover 6 PLs (C, Go, Java, JavaScript, Python, and Rust) and 4 PL pairs (C-Rust, Go-Rust, Java-Python, Python-JavaScript). Our results demonstrate that ReCodeAgent consistently outperforms prior techniques on translation correctness, improving test pass rate by 60.8% on ground-truth tests, with an average cost of $15.3. We also perform process-centric analysis of ReCodeAgent trajectories to confirm its procedural efficiency. Finally, we investigate how the design choices (a multi-agent vs. single-agent architecture) influence ReCodeAgent performance: on average, the test pass rate drops by 40.4%, and trajectories become 28% longer and persistently inefficient.

</details>

### [196] [Content Platform GenAI Regulation via Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06194)
*Wee Chaimanowong*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究生成式AI（GenAI）对内容平台的竞争格局影响，揭示了无监管的GenAI使用会导致内容分发扭曲，降低消费者参与度和平台利润。提出一种基于经济激励的创作者补偿机制，可在无需AI检测器的情况下促进高价值人类内容创作，改善GenAI训练数据污染问题，提升平台效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI（GenAI）的广泛应用引发了两个核心问题：一是原创作者未获得训练数据补偿，二是AI生成内容可能污染训练数据源并替代人类创作，导致内容分发失真，最终损害消费者参与度和平台利润。

Method: 本文采用经济学建模与博弈论分析方法，设计了一个简单的创作者补偿机制，通过经济激励鼓励更多高价值人类内容创作，而非依赖AI内容检测技术。

Result: 所提出的补偿机制能够激励创作者生产更多高价值人类内容，降低训练数据污染，同时提升消费者参与度和平台利润。

Conclusion: 无需AI检测器，基于经济激励的补偿机制可有效平衡GenAI应用与人类创作者利益，改善内容生态系统质量，实现平台、创作者和消费者的多方共赢。

Abstract: The use of Generative AI (GenAI) for creative content generation has gained popularity in recent years. GenAI allows creators to generate contents that are increasingly becoming indistinguishable to the human--generated counter--part at a much lower cost. While GenAI reshapes the competitive landscape of the contents market, the original creators were typically not compensated for their works that were used in the GenAI training. On the other hands, the wide--spread adoption of GenAI threatens to replace the human--generated shares of contents on content platforms, contaminating training data source for future GenAI models. In this paper, we argue that an unregulated usage of GenAI can also be harmful to the platform by causing a contents distribution distortion which can lower the consumers' engagement and the platform's profit. We show that a simple economically--driven creator compensation scheme, can incentivize more creation of high--value human--generated contents, without the need for an AI--detector. This reduces the data pollution for future GenAI training, while improves the consumer engagement and the platform's profit.

</details>


### [197] [Concentrated siting of AI data centers drives regional power-system stress under rising global compute demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06198)
*Danbo Chen,Zijun Zhou,Yongyang Cai,Jiahong Qin,Ani Katchova,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文构建了AI-能源耦合框架，结合大语言模型分析与能源系统建模，预测2025-2030年AI数据中心的电力足迹。研究发现AI基础设施高度集中于北美、西欧和亚太地区（占90%以上），六大领先企业用电量将从2024年的118 TWh增至2030年的239-295 TWh（全球电力需求的约1%）。俄勒冈、弗吉尼亚、爱尔兰等地电网压力指数将超0.25，而德克萨斯和日本等多元化电网能更好承载新增负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能的快速发展导致全球计算需求空前增长，给电力系统带来巨大压力。现有研究缺乏对AI驱动数据中心的系统性电力足迹预测框架。

Method: 构建AI-能源耦合框架：（1）利用大语言模型分析企业、政策、媒体数据；（2）结合定量能源系统建模；（3）预测2025-2030年AI数据中心电力消耗。

Result: AI基础设施高度集中于北美、西欧和亚太（>90%计算容量）；六大企业用电量从2024年118 TWh增至2030年239-295 TWh（约占全球电力需求1%）；俄勒冈、弗吉尼亚、爱尔兰PSI>0.25，电网脆弱；而德克萨斯、日本等多元化电网可有效吸收新增负荷。

Conclusion: AI基础设施正从边缘数字服务演变为电力系统动力学的结构性组件。需要前瞻性规划，将计算增长与可再生能源扩张和电网韧性相结合。

Abstract: The rapid rise of generative artificial intelligence (AI) is driving unprecedented growth in global computational demand, placing increasing pressure on electricity systems. This study introduces an AI-energy coupling framework that combines large language models (LLMs)-based analysis of corporate, policy, and media data with quantitative energy-system modeling to forecast the electricity footprint of AI-driven data centers from 2025 to 2030. Results show that the new AI infrastructure is highly concentrated in North America, Western Europe, and the Asia-Pacific, which together account for more than 90% of projected compute capacity. Aggregate electricity consumption by the six leading firms is projected to increase from roughly 118 TWh in 2024 to between 239 TWh and 295 TWh by 2030, equivalent to about 1% of global power demand. Regions such as Oregon, Virginia, and Ireland may experience high Power Stress Index (PSI) values exceeding 0.25, indicating local grid vulnerability, whereas diversified systems such as those in Texas and Japan can absorb new loads more effectively. These findings demonstrate that AI infrastructure is evolving from a marginal digital service into a structural component of power-system dynamics, underscoring the need for anticipatory planning that aligns computational growth with renewable expansion and grid resilience.

</details>


### [198] [Thinking in Graphs with CoMAP: A Shared Visual Workspace for Designing Project-Based Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06200)
*Ruijia Li,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Designing project-based learning (PBL) demands managing highly interdependent components, a task that both traditional linear tools and purely conversational AI struggle with. Traditional tools fail to capture the non-linear nature of creative design, while conversational systems lack the persistent, shared context necessary for reflective collaboration. Grounded in theories of distributed cognition, we introduce CoMAP, a system that embodies a graph-based collaboration paradigm. By providing a shared visual workspace with dual-modality AI support, CoMAP transforms the human-AI relationship from a prompt-and-response loop into a transparent and equitable partnership. Our study with 30 educators shows CoMAP significantly improves teachers' design expression, divergent thinking, and iterative practice compared to a dialogue-only baseline. These findings demonstrate how a nonlinear, artifact-centric approach can foster trust, reduce cognitive load, and \textcolor{fix}{support} educators to take control of their creative process. Our contributions are available at: https://comap2025.github.io/.

</details>


### [199] [Front-End Ethics for Sensor-Fused Health Conversational Agents: An Ethical Design Space for Biometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06203)
*Hansoo Lee,Rafael A. Calvo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文聚焦于"AI伦理前端设计",探讨传感器融合大语言模型在健康监测中的生物特征翻译伦理问题,提出五维设计空间并建议自适应披露作为安全护栏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究聚焦于后端问题(传感精度、训练数据偏见、多模态融合),忽视了前端问题——不可见的生物特征数据如何被翻译成用户直接体验的语言。传感器数据提供的"客观性幻觉"可能放大AI幻觉风险,将错误转化为有害的医疗指令。

Method: 提出了一个包含五个维度的设计空间:生物特征披露、监控时效性、解释框架、AI立场和可质疑性。分析了这些维度如何与情境(用户发起vs系统发起)交互,并识别了生物反馈循环的风险。

Result: 识别了生物反馈循环风险,提出自适应披露作为安全护栏机制。

Conclusion: 提出"自适应披露"(Adaptive Disclosure)作为安全护栏,并给出设计指南,目标确保健康智能体支持而非破坏用户自主权。

Abstract: The integration of continuous data from built-in sensors and Large Language Models (LLMs) has fueled a surge of "Sensor-Fused LLM agents" for personal health and well-being support. While recent breakthroughs have demonstrated the technical feasibility of this fusion (e.g., Time-LLM, SensorLLM), research primarily focuses on "Ethical Back-End Design for Generative AI", concerns such as sensing accuracy, bias mitigation in training data, and multimodal fusion. This leaves a critical gap at the front end, where invisible biometrics are translated into language directly experienced by users. We argue that the "illusion of objectivity" provided by sensor data amplifies the risks of AI hallucinations, potentially turning errors into harmful medical mandates. This paper shifts the focus to "Ethical Front-End Design for AI", specifically, the ethics of biometric translation. We propose a design space comprising five dimensions: Biometric Disclosure, Monitoring Temporality, Interpretation Framing, AI Stance, and Contestability. We examine how these dimensions interact with context (user- vs. system-initiated) and identify the risk of biofeedback loops. Finally, we propose "Adaptive Disclosure" as a safety guardrail and offer design guidelines to help developers manage fallibility, ensuring that these cutting-edge health agents support, rather than destabilize, user autonomy.

</details>


### [200] [The Human Condition as Reflected in Contemporary Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06206)
*W. Russell Neuman*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究利用六种主流生成式AI模型对人类文化与行为的描述进行交叉分析，发现模型在万亿级token的训练语料中形成了关于人类文化的主题共识，包括叙事意义建构、情感优先认知、联盟心理、地位竞争、威胁敏感性和道德合理化等六大主题，提出LLM可作为"文化凝聚物"的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示透过大型语言模型折射的人类文化潜在结构，探究LLM训练语料所反映的人类文化与行为模式

Method: 使用六个主流生成式模型对同一提示进行平行响应分析，该提示直接询问训练语料对人类文化与行为的揭示。通过跨模型比较，识别反复出现的文化主题共识，并区分模型间差异源于解释框架不同而非实质分歧。

Result: 识别出六大文化主题的稳健跨模型共识：叙事意义建构、情感优先认知、联盟心理、地位竞争、威胁敏感性、道德合理化。各模型差异主要反映不同的解释视角，而非实质性分歧。

Conclusion: LLM作为"文化凝聚物"（cultural condensates）——是人类在数万亿token的聚合交流与叙事中如何描述、辩护和争论自身社会生活的压缩表征。六大主题为心理学、社会学及人类学的进一步研究提供了基础。

Abstract: This study seeks to uncover evidence of a latent structure in evolved human culture as it is refracted through contemporary large language models (LLMs). Drawing on parallel responses from six leading generative models to a prompt which asks directly what their training corpora reveal about human culture and behavior, we identify a robust cross-model consensus on a limited set of recurring cultural themes. The themes include narrative meaning-making, affect-first cognition, coalition psychology, status competition, threat sensitivity, and moral rationalization. Each provides grounds for further psychological and sociological inquiry. There is strong evidence of a convergence in these pattern recognition exercises as differences among models are shown to reflect varying explanatory lenses rather than substantive disagreement. We review these findings in the light of the evolving literatures of moral psychology, evolutionary psychology, anthropology, and the computer science literature on large-scale language modeling. We argue that LLMs function as cultural condensates -- compressed representations of how humans describe, justify, and contest their own social lives across trillions of tokens of aggregated communication and narration.

</details>


### [201] [From experimentation to engagement: on the paradox of participatory AI and power in contexts of forced displacement and humanitarian crises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06219)
*Stella Suge,Sarah W. Spencer,Nyalleng Moorosi,Helen McElhinney,Geoff Loane,Sue Black*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文批判性审视了在人道主义危机和流离失所背景下参与式AI方法的局限性。基于肯尼亚卡库马难民营的试点研究，发现参与式AI方法存在"参与漂洗"和算法伤害的风险，这些风险主要源于人道主义部门内部根深蒂固的权力不对等，而非社区对AI认知不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前参与式AI研究主要集中于全球北方，在全球南方尤其是人道主义危机和被迫流离失所情境中的探索极为有限，但AI和算法工具在此类场景中的应用却在加速。作者试图填补这一研究空白，并质疑现有参与式AI方法在脆弱人群中的适用性。

Method: 在肯尼亚西北部卡库马难民营与受影响社区开展试点调研，收集并分析流离失所和危机影响社区对AI的看法与态度数据。

Result: 研究发现部分参与式AI方法存在重要局限，在人道主义情境中使用可能增加"参与漂洗"和算法伤害风险。这些风险并非主要由不同水平的AI理解程度驱动，而更与嵌入人道主义部门的根本权力结构密切相关。

Conclusion: 作者主张需要更严格的参与式方法以及能够问责人道主义AI的独立治理架构。核心论点是将责任归结为个人认知差异具有误导性——结构性权力失衡（援助接受者与服务提供者、捐赠国与东道国、AI公司与人道主义行动者之间的权力差异）才是根本症结所在。

Abstract: Across the Global North, calls for participatory artificial intelligence (AI) to improve the responsible, safe, and ethical use of AI have increased, particularly efforts that engage citizens and communities whose well-being and safety may be directly impacted by AI and other algorithmic tools. These initiatives include surveys, community consultations, citizens' councils and assemblies, and co-designing AI models and projects. Far fewer efforts, however, have been made in the Global South, particularly in contexts related to humanitarian crises and forced displacement, where the deployment of AI and algorithmic tools is accelerating. In this paper, we critically examine participatory AI methods and their limitations in these contexts and explore the opinions and perceptions of AI held by displaced and crisis-affected communities. Based on a pilot exercise with communities living in Kakuma Refugee Camp in northwestern Kenya, we find important limitations in some participatory AI approaches which, if used in humanitarian contexts, could increase risks of so-called 'participation washing' and algorithmic harm. We argue that these risks are not predominantly driven by varying levels of understanding and awareness of AI but more closely linked to the fundamental power dynamics embedded within the humanitarian sector: between humanitarian aid recipients, service providers, donor governments, and host nations, as well as the power differentials and incentives that exist between AI companies and humanitarian actors. These structural conditions make the case not only for more rigorous participatory methods, but for independent governance architecture capable of holding humanitarian AI to account.

</details>


### [202] [The End of Human Judgment in the Kill Chain? Relocating Initiative and Interpretation with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06300)
*Jovana Davidovic*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文论证了大型语言模型（LLM）智能体在战场上的核心功能（如情报分析、数据融合、战场管理）应用中，其固有的主动性、解释能力、目标导向性和动态记忆特性，与人类判断与控制的有效性存在根本矛盾，从而使其与现行国际治理框架（如GGE-CCW和REAIM）的要求不相容。作者认为，在当前及可预见的条件下，用于致命场景中数据融合和战斗管理的自主性AI应用无法在战场上被合理使用，并就此提出了两项国际治理建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体正被整合到战场核心功能中，而现有的国际治理框架（如GGE-CCW和REAIM）建立在"人类判断与控制"这一核心原则之上。作者认为现有框架未能充分审视LLM智能体所带来的结构性挑战——即其运作特征本身可能使人类控制变得实质上无效，而非仅仅是形式上存在。

Method: 本文采用案例分析方法（use cases），具体分析LLM智能体在情报分析、数据融合和战场管理等任务中的运作机制，揭示其"主动性、解释能力、目标导向性和动态记忆"四大特征如何系统性地削弱人类决策者在杀伤链（kill chain）中的实质性控制能力。

Result: 研究结果表明：（1）LLM智能体因重新配置了主动性和解释权，实质性地排斥了人类决策者；（2）这种排斥使得其在致命场景中的应用与现有治理框架的要求不相容；（3）在数据融合和战斗管理等特定应用场景下，当前和可预见的条件下无法实现合理的战场部署。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：应用于致命场景中数据融合和战斗管理的一类自主性AI应用，在现有及可预见的治理条件下，无法在战场上获得正当性使用依据。论文最后就国际治理社区如何应对这一挑战提出了两项具体建议。

Abstract: Large language model-based agents are increasingly being integrated into core battlefield functions, including intelligence analysis, data fusion, and battlefield management.
