We instantiate this protocol in a declarative runtime and evaluate it on noisy Collaborative Battleship [4] using four progressively structured agents over 54 games (18 boards $\times$ 3 seeds).
  The resulting decomposition isolates four components: posterior belief tracking, explicit world-model planning, symbolic in-episode reflection, and sparse LLM-based revision. Across this decomposition, explicit world-model planning improves substantially over a greedy posterior-following baseline (+24.1pp win rate, +0.017 F1). Symbolic reflection operates as a real runtime mechanism -- with prediction tracking, confidence gating, and guarded revision actions -- even though its current revision presets are not yet net-positive in aggregate. Adding conditional LLM revision at about 4.3\% of turns yields only a small and non-monotonic change: average F1 rises slightly (+0.005) while win rate drops (31$\rightarrow$29 out of 54).
  These results suggest a methodological contribution rather than a leaderboard claim: externalizing reflection turns otherwise latent agent behavior into inspectable runtime structure, allowing the marginal role of LLM intervention to be studied directly.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [Logical Robots: Declarative Multi-Agent Programming in Logica](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06629)
*Evgeny Skvortsov,Yilin Xia,Ojaswa Garg,Shawn Bowers,Bertram Ludäscher*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Logical Robots是一个交互式多智能体仿真平台，使用逻辑编程语言Logica以声明式方式指定自主机器人行为。通过逻辑谓词将雷达阵列观测和共享内存映射到期望的电机输出，实现低层反应式控制与高层规划的统一编程环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人行为编程中，低层反应式控制与高层规划通常使用不同工具和语言实现，缺乏统一框架，导致系统复杂性增加、代码维护困难。需要一个能够同时支持反应式控制和规划的多智能体机器人仿真平台。

Method: 采用逻辑编程语言Logica作为行为规范的核心，通过逻辑谓词定义从传感器观测（雷达阵列、共享内存）到执行器输出（电机控制）的映射关系，实现声明式编程范式。

Result: 提供了一个能够同时支持低层反应式控制和高层规划的多智能体仿真平台，机器人行为可以在单一编程环境中以声明式方式定义和组合。

Conclusion: Logical Robots证明了逻辑编程可作为多智能体机器人行为规范的有效框架，为探索多智能体机器人行为提供了统一、一致的编程环境。

Abstract: We present Logical Robots, an interactive multi-agent simulation platform where autonomous robot behavior is specified declaratively in the logic programming language Logica. Robot behavior is defined by logical predicates that map observations from simulated radar arrays and shared memory to desired motor outputs. This approach allows low-level reactive control and high-level planning to coexist within a single programming environment, providing a coherent framework for exploring multi-agent robot behavior.

</details>


### [240] [Strategic Persuasion with Trait-Conditioned Multi-Agent Systems for Iterative Legal Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07028)
*Philipp D. Siedler*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Strategic Courtroom Framework，一个多智能体模拟环境，利用大语言模型(LLM)模拟控辩双方的法庭论证过程。通过9个可解释特质构建四种原型队伍，在7000+场模拟审判中发现异质性团队表现优于同质化配置，中等交互深度产生更稳定判决，量化型和魅力型特质贡献最大，并提出基于强化学习的特质编排器可动态生成超越人工设计的防御策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统博弈论模型抽象掉了语言说服机制，而法律、外交、谈判等对抗性领域的战略互动正是通过话语实现的。研究旨在弥合语言作为战略工具与计算框架之间的鸿沟，使语言成为一等战略行动空间。

Method: 1) 构建包含9个可解释特质的4种原型多智能体系统; 2) 使用DeepSeek-R1和Gemini-2.5-Pro在10个合成案例、84种三人队伍配置下进行7000+场模拟审判; 3) 开发基于强化学习的Trait Orchestrator动态生成防御特质策略。

Result: 异质性团队显著优于同质化配置; 中等交互深度产生更稳定判决; 量化型和魅力型特质对说服成功贡献突出; 动态生成的特质策略可超越静态人工设计组合。

Conclusion: 语言可作为一等战略行动空间; 该框架为构建自适应说服型自主智能体奠定基础; 为多智能体环境中研究话语说服机制提供可扩展平台。

Abstract: Strategic interaction in adversarial domains such as law, diplomacy, and negotiation is mediated by language, yet most game-theoretic models abstract away the mechanisms of persuasion that operate through discourse. We present the Strategic Courtroom Framework, a multi-agent simulation environment in which prosecution and defense teams composed of trait-conditioned Large Language Model (LLM) agents engage in iterative, round-based legal argumentation. Agents are instantiated using nine interpretable traits organized into four archetypes, enabling systematic control over rhetorical style and strategic orientation.
  We evaluate the framework across 10 synthetic legal cases and 84 three-trait team configurations, totaling over 7{,}000 simulated trials using DeepSeek-R1 and Gemini~2.5~Pro. Our results show that heterogeneous teams with complementary traits consistently outperform homogeneous configurations, that moderate interaction depth yields more stable verdicts, and that certain traits (notably quantitative and charismatic) contribute disproportionately to persuasive success. We further introduce a reinforcement-learning-based Trait Orchestrator that dynamically generates defense traits conditioned on the case and opposing team, discovering strategies that outperform static, human-designed trait combinations.
  Together, these findings demonstrate how language can be treated as a first-class strategic action space and provide a foundation for building autonomous agents capable of adaptive persuasion in multi-agent environments.

</details>


### [241] [Intertemporal Demand Allocation for Inventory Control in Online Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07312)
*Rene Caldentey,Tong Xie*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究在线市场平台如何通过跨时期间的需求分配策略来影响卖家的库存决策，在不直接控制库存的情况下实现对卖家的间接管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线市场平台日益超越简单的买卖匹配功能，通过路由订单和提供配套履约服务创造收益。然而，平台如何在不直接控制库存的前提下影响卖家的库存决策，这一问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 构建理论模型：平台观察总需求、跨时间分配订单给卖家；卖家在两种履约方式（商家自履FBM和平台履约FBP）间选择，同时在状态依赖的基础库存策略下补货。核心研究机制是信息性的：平台通过改变每个卖家销售流的可预测性，即使平均需求份额不变，也能改变卖家的安全库存需求。

Result: 在同等待遇分配政策类中：1）均匀分配最小化预测不确定性；2）任何更高水平的不确定性可通过简单的低记忆分配规则实现；3）高于均匀基准的不确定性增加需要路由规则来阻止卖家从自身销售历史中推断总需求。

Conclusion: 平台 fulfillment 采用率与采用者的库存水平之间存在权衡关系。需求分配是数字市场中强大的运营和信息设计杠杆。

Abstract: Online marketplaces increasingly do more than simply match buyers and sellers: they route orders across competing sellers and, in many categories, offer ancillary fulfillment services that make seller inventory a source of platform revenue. We investigate how a platform can use intertemporal demand allocation to influence sellers' inventory choices without directly controlling stock. We develop a model in which the platform observes aggregate demand, allocates orders across sellers over time, and sellers choose between two fulfillment options, fulfill-by-merchant (FBM) and fulfill-by-platform (FBP), while replenishing inventory under state-dependent base-stock policies. The key mechanism we study is informational: by changing the predictability of each seller's sales stream, the platform changes sellers' safety-stock needs even when average demand shares remain unchanged. We focus on nondiscriminatory allocation policies that give sellers the same demand share and forecast risk. Within this class, uniform splitting minimizes forecast uncertainty, whereas any higher level of uncertainty can be implemented using simple low-memory allocation rules. Moreover, increasing uncertainty above the uniform benchmark requires routing rules that prevent sellers from inferring aggregate demand from their own sales histories. These results reduce the platform's problem to choosing a level of forecast uncertainty that trades off adoption of platform fulfillment against the inventory held by adopters. Our analysis identifies demand allocation as a powerful operational and informational design lever in digital marketplaces.

</details>
